3a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dpa' chen po//'phags pa spyan ras gzi dbang

3b1(rgyud): hi ma la ya'i tshal chen po//tsan dan gyi sdong po dang /myu gu dang /me tog rgyas pa'i

3b3(rgyud): lha dang mi la sogs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi tshogs chen po dang thabs gcig tu

4a1(rgyud): @#/ O /srungs dang /tshe dang ldan pa rlangs pa dang /tshe dang ldan pa kun shes 
4a2(rgyud): ko'u din+ya dang /tshe dang ldan pa ma 'gags pa dang /tshe dang ldan ba sha ri'i bu dang /tshe dang  
4a3(rgyud): ldan pa mo'u 'gal gyi bu chen po dang /tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la sogs pa/dge

4b1(rgyud): chen po la 'di lta ste/pad ma dkar po dang /nyi 'od dang /gzungs kyi dbang phyug rgyal po dang /pad ma'i spyan 
4b2(rgyud): dang /ye shes dbang phyug dang /pad ma'i dbang phyug dang /rdo rje snying po dang /rin chen snying po dang /kun tu bzang 
4b3(rgyud): po dang /sgrib pa rnam par sel dang /nam mkha'i snying po dang /byams pa dang /sa'i snying po dang /'jam dpal 
4b4(rgyud): dang /'jig rten dpal dang /phyag na rdo rje dang /blo gros mi zad pa dang /nam mkha'i mdzod dang /blo gros rgya 
4b5(rgyud): mtsho dang /mthu chen po thob pa dang /zla 'od dang /dra ba can gyi 'od dang /spobs pa brtsegs pa dang /mya ngan dang 
5a1(rgyud): @#/ O /mun pa thams cad sel ba'i blo gros la sogs pa/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
5a2(rgyud): chen po grongs med pa dang /gzhan yang gcor byams pa la gnas pa'i dge slong mang po dang /dge slong ma mang po dang /
5a3(rgyud): dge bsnyen dang /dge bsnyen ma mang po dang /lha'i tshogs dang /klu'i tshogs dang /de bzhin du gnod sbyin dang /dri 
5a4(rgyud): za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /grul bum dang /sha za dang /
5a5(rgyud): mi dang mi ma yin pa bye ba phrag drug dang /khro bo chen po rdo rje hUM mdzad dang /rdo rje gshin rje gshed dang /rdo rje gzhan gyis 
5b1(rgyud): mi thub pa dang /rdo rje rta mgrin dang /rdo rje bgegs mthar byed dang /rdo rje gtsug tor dang /rdo rje gnod mdzes rgyal po la sogs pa bdun cu dang /spyan 
5b2(rgyud): ma dang /ma mA ki dang /na bza' dkar mo dang /sgrol ma dang /'od zer can dang /dbyangs can ma dang /phags zhal ma dang /khro gnyer can dang /ri khrod 
5b3(rgyud): ma dang /dug sel ma dang /dkar mo dang /yid bzangs ma dang /rnam par rgyal ma dang /dpal phreng ma dang /sa 'dzin bzang mo dang /rdo rje dbang 
5b4(rgyud): phyug ma la sogs pa stong drug brgya dang thabs cig tu bzhugs so//de'i tshe 'khor de dag thams cad kyis/bcom ldan 'das bla ma la bkur sti dang rim 'gro'i 
5b5(rgyud): phyir/mchod pa du mas mchod (cing)bsngags pa du mas bstod cing bskor bar byed do//de nas bcom ldan 'das thub pa chen po//thugs rje chen po yongs su 'byung 
5b6(rgyud): ba zhes bya'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu sa chen po rnam pa drug tu g.yos shing ltas bco brgyad du gyur to////
6a1(rgyud): @#/ O /de'i ltas mthong ba lang ka'i bdag po sgra sgrogs shin tu snyems pa dang ldan par gyur nas/rang gi 'khor du ma dang der lhags so//
6a2(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis sgra sgrogs kyi sems kyi rtog pa thugs su chud nas/sku'i gnas thams cad nas 'od zer bye ba phrag du ma byung ngo //'od zer de dang /sa 
6a3(rgyud): g.yos pa de dag gis/phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyang gsal bar gyur to//de'i tshe sgra sgrogs bsnyems pa dang bcas par de'i 'khor thams cad kyis/
6a4(rgyud): 'dzam bu'i gling du yams nad la sogs pas rnam par 'tshe ba dang /thugs kyis yongs su dgongs nas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras 
6a5(rgyud): gzigs dbang phyug gdong bcu gcig pa/drang srong rig sngags 'chang bye ba phyag brgya stong dang lhan cig bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar lhags nas zhabs la spyi bos phyag   
6a6(rgyud): 'tshal te/g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//de nas sangs rgyas kyi mthus/lha'i tshogs rnams kyi nang nas lha'i bu rin po che pad ma'i 
6b1(rgyud): dra bas brgyan pa zhes bya zhing sa te thal mo sbyar nas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la 'di skad ces smras so//kye ma
6b2(rgyud): mgon po thugs rje can//rigs sngags 'chang ba'i tshogs dang bcas//che ba'i byin brlabs ldan pa rnams//sgra sgrogs bsnyems pa gzhom pa dang //nyon mongs rtsad 
6b3(rgyud): nas gcod pa dang //sems can rnams la phan pa dang //bdag la shin tu sman pa'i phyir//sku gdung rjes mthun pa yi//rig sngags sbyor ba khyad par can//snying po   
6b4(rgyud): dam pa bshad du gsol//de tshe 'khor tshogs thams cad kyis//mkha' phyogs me tog char gyi bkang //bcom ldan thub pa'i dbu la ni//rin chen me tog gdugs su 
6b5(rgyud): gyur//lha rnams gtsang mar sbyar ba yi//yon chab bdug tog me dri ba shos//ci 'byor dad pa'i bstobs kyis phul//thub pas namakha'i mdzod du brlabs//lha yi 
6b6(rgyud): bu mo du ma yis//rol mo'i sil snyan 'phan gdugs dang //bla bre rgyal mtshan lhag lhub dang //lda ldi na bza' du mas mchod//de nas mgon po spyan ras gzigs//
6b7(rgyud): pad ma gzhon nu'i zhal mnga' bas//bzhad 'dzum thal mo sbyar nas ni//ston pa la ni 'di skad gsol//sgra sgrogs bzhin gyis bsnyems pa dang //mda' dang rtsal gyis     
7a1(rgyud): dregs pa ste//'di ni legs brtsams las byung min//shin tu log pa las kyang min//sngon du sangs rgyas gdong mdzes kyis//bstan 
7a2(rgyud): pa la yang mgo btang ba'i//bsod nams grangs kyis 'byung ba yin//lag pa du mar 'phrul pa ni//zas dang snod spyad gos nod rgyan//bskal pa bzhi bcu'i mchod 
7a3(rgyud): sbyin te//de nas rtsal dang shul dang yin//mnga' ni snying gi riDa yi//byin brlabs mngon du byang mod kyang //grangs mang tshul khrims 'dral bas ni//srin po'i rigs 
7a4(rgyud): su 'khrungs pa dang //'khor bcas gzhan la gcod 'gyur yin//ngas ni dang tshul rmad byung ba'i//lus kyis bde gshegs mchod sbyin dang //bsgrub brtson legs byas 
7a5(rgyud): mtha' yas pa//spyod bzang la gnas bskal pa'i grangs//gang ga klung gi rdul dang mtshungs//de snyed 'bras bu de tsam ste//dang po las can ji snyed la//phan bdags 
7a6(rgyud): par ni bya ba'i phyir//bcung zad bsdus nas brjod bya ste//lags par nyon la yid la zung //sngon tshe 'das dus bskal pa ni//gang ga klung bcu'i snga rol gyi//cher 'das 
7a7(rgyud): pa yi snga rol na//bde gshegs rdzogs sangs rgyas kyi mnan//man d+ha ra yi dri zhes bya//'jig rten ston pa 'byung ba'i tshe//bdag ni khyim bdag grags par 'gyur//thub 
7b1(rgyud): pa de las rig sngags mnos//thob ma thag tu bskal pa'i grangs//bzhi khri snyed kyi 'khor ba bsnyil//de rjes bskal pa gang ga'i klung //bye ma snyed cig sngon rol na//
7b2(rgyud): bde gshegs rdzogs sangs rgyas kyi mtshan//thogs med pad ma brgya'i spyan//gtsug tor yon tan rab rgyas pa'i//'od zer rgyal po zhes bya ba//'jig rten ston 
7b3(rgyud): par bzhugs pa'i tshe//bdag nyid drang srong bdag por//rig sngags 'di mnos bsgrub pa'i yais//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas zhal mthong zhing //skye med chos la 
7b4(rgyud): bzod pa thob//de rjes 'das dus bskal pa ni//bye ba phrag brgya'i snga rol na//bde gshegs rdzogs sangs rgya kyi mtshan//tshe dpag med ces 'jig rten du//ston 
7b5(rgyud): par 'byung la bdag gis mnos//de tshe phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyis//bdag la dbang bskur lung bstan mdzad//khyod kyis sngon du bsngal pa'i grangs//brjod mi 
7b6(rgyud): lang nas rig sngags phyir//yang dag stong phrag stong snyed kyang //dang pa'i mchod sbyin chen por byas//bye ba phrag grangs bcu gcig tu//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgya 
7b7(rgyud): dbang bskur blangs//de dang de yi rnam smin mchog/sangs rgyas zhing khams rab 'byam du//yang dang yang du 'byung bar 'gyur//phyis ni 'dren pa shAkya zhes//mi mjed 
8a1(rgyud): 'jig rten khams 'khrungs tshe//mi yul las kyang khyad par du//yi dgas gling du skyes pa 'ga'//bstan la dad 'dun 'phel bya'i//
8a2(rgyud): mtshan mo mun nag sprin rum du//ska cig glog 'gyu ji lta bar//rig sngags 'chang ba gdong bcu gcig/yan lag stong dang ldan pa la//spyan ni tshig stong suM bcu pa'i//mtshan 
8a3(rgyud): dang ldan pa'i sku 'byung 'gyur//de tshe ska ldan la la zhig/mtshan nas brjod cing sku la mos//rig sngags zlos pa po gang zhig/'jig rten 'dod pa kun 'grub cing //phyi nas 
8a4(rgyud): ye shes nyi shar 'gyur//lung bstan de skad thob pa'i phyir//sangs rgyas bye ba phrag bcu gcig/grangs snyed zhal nas gsungs pa yi//rig sngags snying po gdong bcu 
8a5(rgyud): gcig/mthu chen thogs pa med pa ste//'gro la sman zhing bde ba dang //nad kun sel byed sdig kun sbyong //bkra shis med pa kun bzlog cing //rmi lam mtshan ma ngan 
8a6(rgyud): kun sel//dus min 'chi dang ngan sems bzlog/rtag tu dpal dang sems bde thob//mya ngan sdug bsngal ngal ba sel//rtsod 'khrug thams cad zhi ba dang //bdud dang 
8a7(rgyud): gdon bgegs zhi bar 'gyur//'jig rten smon pa kun 'grub cing //bdud dang bar chod mtshon nad dang //dbyig pa brdeg dang bcing pa dang //brtson rar bcug dang mtshon char 
8b1(rgyud): bstsal//gsod sar khrid dang yan lag gcod//mig 'bru mtshon gyis gtse ba dang /chu dang me dang rlung sa g.yo'//dug dang ro langs byad sdems dang //chom rkun mi ma yin 
8b2(rgyud): rnams gnon//rig sngags 'di yis bsrung ba dang /len dang 'dzin dang 'bri ba dang //bklag dang kha ton bshad sogs rnams//brtson la 'jig rten lha rnams dang //bdud 
8b3(rgyud): dang tshangs pa dge sbyong dang //bram ze bcas pas gnod pa med//sngon gyi las drag yod pa ni//byang zhing srabs te med par 'gyur//nges pa'i dngos po 'di lags par//
8b4(rgyud): rig nas mngon sum yid ches par//rnam par rtog med sems blang (sku)//de slad skyabs dang skyob pa dang //bsrung dang dbugs 'byin dpal mgon dang //gling dang khang khyim 
8b5(rgyud): 'di nyid yin//nye rang sa phyogs ji srid par//'tshams cad na ni rig sngags dang //'byung po rnams kyis 'tshe ba med//gang gis sangs rgyas phyag 'tshal mchod//skyabs 
8b6(rgyud): su 'gro zhing sdig pa bshags //mngon par dga' zhing gzungs sngags 'di//nyi shu rtsa gcig brjod pa la//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas ma lus pa//mtshungs par yid rang leg+sa 
8b7(rgyud): so sbyin//rtsa ba bzhi dang 'tshams med lnga//ma lus sdig kun byang bar 'gyur//gnod sbyin srin po sogs snang yang //gzungs nas byams pa snying rje'i sems//dang po bskyed nas 
9a1(rgyud): @#//bla med kyi//byang chub mchog gi sa la dgod//gang ga klung brgya'i sangs rgyas kyi//mtshan nas brjod cing bsdod pa bas//rigs kyi bu 'am bu 
9a2(rgyud): mo gang //bdag gi ming brjod dran byed cing //rig sngags rgyun du brjod na de//myur du mchog dga'i sa thob cing //lag pa'i mthil na mthong ba ltar//bla med byang chub myur 
9a3(rgyud): du 'byung //rtse cig dran pas gus byed te//rtag tu nang par langs nas//cho ga bzhin du dag bgyis te//brgya rtsa brgyad du gang brjod pa//tshe 'dir phan yon bcu yang thob//rtag 
9a4(rgyud): par lus la nad med cing //lha dang sangs rgyas kun gyis bsrung //nor 'bru rin chen gos rnyed dang //dgra sdang dul zhing 'jigs med dang //bza' rnams bkur cing rigs 
9a5(rgyud): btsun 'thob//dug dang 'dre srin gdon gnod bral//lus la dri zhim kun dga' dang //gtam ngan mi snyam brjod byed dang //rims nad bral zhing dgang sla dang //dus min 
9a6(rgyud): 'chi ba spong bar 'gyur//gzhan yang yon tan bzhi thob ste//'chi kar sangs rgyas zhal mthong dang //mkha' la 'di 'byung lhas mchod dang //nams kyang ngan 'gror mi 'gro 
9a7(rgyud): dang //bde ba can nam bde 'gror skye//rig sngags dang sangs rgyas kyi//ye shes snying po 'dzin pa la//byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi//rnam par thar pa'i chos 
9b1(rgyud): sgo thob//bskal pa du mar dge bsags dang //rig sngags 'di rnyed 'gyur ba ste//dad ldan khrel yod ngo tsha shes//blo bzang brtson ldan snying rje che//mdog dkar dang bzang gsod
9b2(rgyud): por smra//sems 'jam lus ngag dal bar spyod//bla ma la gus brtul zhugs che//sred pa chung zhing sbyin la dga'//sangs rgyas la gus sems bcas pas//nyin mtshan
9b3(rgyud): rgyun du rig sngags 'don//bcu bzhi 'am ni bco lnga dang //khyad par du yang tshes brgyad la//gso sbyong sku yi mdun du blangs//skyon med tsan dan dkar sogs shing //rigs
9b4(rgyud): bzang las byas sku gzugs sam//yang na sa rdo che la sogs//bzang las bde bar gshegs gzugs bya//yang na ris su bris kyang rung //sku cig pu 'am lha rdzas kyi//dkyil
9b5(rgyud): 'khor rgyas par byas pa la//mngon sum bzhin du dmigs byas la//myur du mngon sum mthong bar 'gyur//yon tan kun gyis brgyan pa yi//bum pa chu dang bcas pa la//sngags
9b6(rgyud): brjod pas ni 'dod don kun//grub 'gyur the tshom mi bya'o//nad dang 'phrod pa'i sman rnams la//bsngags la bza' 'am lus la byug/bdugs pa tsam yang gnod pa sel//srang
9b7(rgyud): bu 'am ni gos dag dang //phyag rgyas btab pa 'ang de bzhin no//'dod pa gang dang gang la yang //ge'i las kyis sbyar bar bya//gsang sngags 'dom 'dzin yid byed la//bdag kyang rjes
10a1(rgyud): pad dra

10a7(rgyud): ma byung ma skyes ma 'gags pa//skye 'gag lta bu'i cho 'phrul ni//sku dang phyag rgya sngags su snang //yon tan chu yi rlabs bzhin du//skye ba'i ngo bo gsal bar byed//nan tan 
10a8(rgyud): gyis ni de nyid kyang //rgyu ba med par khong du chud//byang chub sems dpa' sa snying gis//rgyu la ched du 'di brjod do//e ma rang bzhin rnam dag la//thog med blo yi mun pas 
10b1(rgyud): dbyings//rnam dag bag chags kyis dkrugs nas//blo bur dkar gnag las su snang //dkar po sangs rgyas chos dge 'dun//de la sgro btags byas sems dang //de las 
10b2(rgyud): byung ba'i phrin las kyis//nyon mongs las kyi sgrib sbyong byed//mgon po byams pas sngags sgrub kyi//lam la ched du 'di ltar brjod//e ma gdod nas grol zhi'i sems//
10b3(rgyud): chu ltar dang bar snang mod kyang //sna tshogs 'gro ba'i gzugs 'byung bas//mun pa'i thibs pos rnam par bsgyur//de nyid sems kyi snang ba'i phyir//'bad rtsol med pa'i seMs 
10b4(rgyud): kyis grol//rtsol ba sems kyi dri mas ni//dri ma med pa'i sems 'ching 'gyur//nam mkha'i snying pos sngags ming la//ched du brjod pa 'di ltar brjod//e mastong pa mtshan 
10b5(rgyud): ma bral//kha dog dbyibs bral chos nyid bdag/dga' ba'i sems chen gsal byed pa//shes rab chen po yi ge man//mtshan byed bde ldan ye shes sku//gang gang 
10b6(rgyud): don du de snang ba//snying rje chen po'i rang shugs kyis//'byung 'gyur ye ge tra yi don//byang chub sems dpa' pad mos ni//gnas la ched du 'di ltar brjod //e ma rgyu rkyen 
10b7(rgyud): nam mkha' la//nyi zla rgyu skar 'khor ba yis//gsal nas nyin mtshan bye brag brjod//nyon mongs ma rig srid len gyis//'du byed srid pa'i las bsgrubs te//rnam shes ming gzugs 
11a1(rgyud): skye mched drugreg tshor skye rga shi sdug bsngal//gsal byed dag pa nyid dag ni phyag na rdo rjes 'bras don la//ched du brjod pa 'di brjod do//e ma zab 
11a2(rgyud): cing rgya che ba//sngags mthu bla na med pa ni//sangs rgyas mu stegs kyis mi shes//shes pa nyid kyang rdzas med la//shes bya rdzas su ga la yod//shes dang yul gyi rnam par ni//

11a4(rgyud): mgon po spyan ras gzigs la bstod//oM bcom ldan bla ma mgon po spyan ras gzigs//zhal grangs mdog dbyibs du ma cir yang snang //stong dang brgya dang bcu gcig drug dang 
11a5(rgyud): gcig/byams pa'i zhal bzang mnga' la phyag 'tshal lo//spyan ma'i 'o//oM rab dges zhal mdog du ma cir yang 'gyur//la la byams pa snying rje dga' ba'i zhal//la la 
11a6(rgyud): 'dod rmongs ser sna chen po'i zhal//khro sdang gtum chags ldan la phyag 'tshal lo//ma ma kis so//oM phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas dbang bskur rdzogs pa'i zhal/btan pa med pa'i 
11a7(rgyud): mchod sbyin rgyas pa'i phyagmdzad pa grangs med chub pa'i phyag rgyar ldan//bden gnyis thugs su gshegs la phyag 'tshal lo//na bza' dkar mo'i 'o//oM mdzad pa thugs rje'i 
11b1(rgyud): dbyings la thugs tsam pa rgya mtsho rgyun mi 'chad mdzad pas/zhing rnams rgya mtsho rnam par sbyong ba yi//mgon po spyan ras gzigs la phyag 'tshal lo//sgrol ma'i 'o//oM nyi ma gzi ldan 
11b2(rgyud): 'od zer 'gyed pa ltar 'jug dgongs mi mnga' kun rtog mun bral ba'i/skal pa rgya mtshar khyad par don gsal ba'i//mgon po spyan ras gzi la phyag 'tshal lo//'od zer 
11b3(rgyud): can gyi'o//oM sems kyi rgyud 'gags mya ngan 'das pa che//'dod chags byung ma thag du yal ba ste//nyon mongs rtsa ba bsal nas pha mtha' med//mgon po spyan ras gzig+sa 
11b4(rgyud): la phyag 'tshal lo//dbyangs can no//oM nyon mongs bdag tu bzung nas 'khor bar skya//de la yang yang brtags na ngan 'gror skye//rang bzhin med par shes na grol bar 'gyur//
11b5(rgyud): mgon po spyan ras gzigasal phyag 'tshal lo//phag gdong ma'i 'o//oM chos rnams dag par mthong na mtho ris thob//gnyis 'dzin rtog pa chad na mya ngan 'das//sgyu ma'i 
11b6(rgyud): sems dang ldan na gzhan don 'byung //mgon po spyan ras gzigs la phyag 'tshal lo//khro gnyer ma'i 'o//oM rang tshul spyod la chags pas sa bon rul//gzhal tshul 
11b7(rgyud): dman par brjod pas zhing sa tshig/lus ngag gtsang sbra tsam la byang chung ring //mgon po spyan ras gzigs pa la phyag 'tshal lo//sa 'dzin ma'i 'o//oM bdag lta mun pa'i dug gis 
12a1(rgyud): @#/ O /brgyal ba la//bdag med phyogs bral ye shes bdud rtsi'i sman//pha rol phyin bcu spyod dang rtogs rtsol mdzad//mgon po spyan ras gzigs 
12a2(rgyud): la phyag 'tshal lo//dug sel ma yi'o//oM rang tshul bzang spyod nga rgyal zhen pa med//gzhan spyod btang snyoms byas nas sems sbyong //sred med bzod ldan ngang gi dgon 
12a3(rgyud): pa lags//mgon po spyan ras gzis la phyag 'tshal lo//ri khrod ma yi'o//oM khrel yod ngo tsha shes shing gtong la dga' dad dang thos ldan tshul khrims shes rab bzang //
12a4(rgyud): cho ga shes 'dod chung 'di dang gzhan du phyag/mgon po spyan ras gzig la phyag 'tshal lo//nor rgyun ma'i 'o//oM yi ge 'bri mchod sbyin nyan klog dang 'chad//kha don 
12a5(rgyud): 'dzin sems bsgom brtson de la chu'i//khrus sogs yungs kar gtsang sogs yon tan bstsol//mgon po spyan ras gzi gas la phyag 'tshal lo//dkar mo'i yod/
12a6(rgyud): oM rang sems dul zhing lus ngag bag yod spyod//'jig rten 'jig rten 'das mchod dam pa 'dzin//thos bsam bsgom brtson dgra las rnam rgyal ba'i//mgon po spyan   
12a7(rgyud): ras gzigs la phyag 'tshal lo//rnam rgyal ma'i glu'o//oM tha dad med chos bya byed grol ba'i rgyu//thabs dang shes rab bde chen byang chub lam//rnam thar ye shes mtha' 
12b1(rgyud): yas dpal ster ba'i//mgon po spyan ras gzigs la phyag 'tshal lo//dpal phreng ma'i glu'o//oM bsam pa bzang po gser gyi sa gzhi la//byang chub phyogs chos dpa' bsaM 
12b2(rgyud): shing chen 'khrungs//ye shes thugs rje'i chu yis rlan mdzad pa'i//mgon po spyan ras gzigs la phyag 'tshal lo//yid bzangs ma'i glu'o//sgra sgrogs 'khor bcas 
12b3(rgyud): 'dar gyur te//rlung gis btab pa'i rtswa dang mtshungs//thal mo spyi bor sbyar nas su//ched du brjed pas 'di gsol to//oM mgon po rang 'byung bcom ldan 'das//gtso bo 
12b4(rgyud): rab tu che ba'i lha//bsod nams du mas skyed pa'i sku//ye shes mtha' yas thugs mnga' ba//blo ldan srid mthar mdzad pa po//tshul ldan tshul khrims la sbyor mdzad//
12b5(rgyud): gtsang ldan bram ze tshang pa che//mdzad spyod mkha' mnyam spyod phyag 'tshal//de nas bcom ldan thub pa ches//'khor la legs so legs so//oM A hi hUM swA hAr bcas/
12b6(rgyud): yang yang brjod pas byin gyis brlabs//lcags kyu can gyis nya ra nas//klu phrug 'bum phrag 'dren pa ltar//dge spyod kun slong dkar po ni//byang chub rin chen gling mchog 
12b7(rgyud): 'dren//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po pad ma dra ba zhes bya ba las////gleng bzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o//
13a1(rgyud): /de nas bcom ldan thub pa che//mgon po yang dag rdzogs sangs rgyas//sna tshogs snang ba zhes bya ba'i//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//
13a2(rgyud): yan lag cha shas thams cad las//'od zer sna lnga bye ba phrag/drug cu drug cu'i mtha' gzhal ba//grangs med dag ni bkye bar mdzad//zhing khams grangs med bdun gyi mtha'//
13a3(rgyud): phyogs bcu ma lus thams cad du//'od kyis gang nas 'od de las//snyan pa'i tshigs bcad 'di snang ngo //sangs rgyas dang ni chos la brlas pa spangs//le lo che dang cung zad chos 
13a4(rgyud): 'dzin spang //'gyod dang chos la ma nges byed pa spang //nga rgyal che dang 'dod chags spyod pa spang //'gro rnams med na byang chub lam med te//'gro la rten nas byang chub che thob 'gyur//
13a5(rgyud): byang chub che lam thugs rje chen po ste//'gro rnams med na gang la skye bar 'gyur//de skad grag pa'i de ma thag/phyogs bcu'i zhing bzhugs sangs rgyas sras//brjod kyis mi lang 
13a6(rgyud): gtso bo mchog/spyan ras gzigs dbang grags chen gtso//thams cad 'khor dang bcas pa rnams//mi mjed zhing 'dir lhags par gyur//'di nas shar phyogs zhing khams ni//
13a7(rgyud): grangs med bdun gyi mtha' nyid nas//zhing khams seng ge 'phungs par ni//sangs rgyas seng ge rol pa yi//drung nas byang chub sems dpa'i gtso//spyan ras gzigs dbang seng 
13b1(rgyud): gi sgra//mdog dkar shel gyi ri 'dra ba//byang chub sems mchog bye ba phrag/bcu dang lhan cig mkha' la gshegs//glog 'gyu bzhin du zhing 'di byon//bcom ldan mgon 
13b2(rgyud): po thub pa la//phyag 'tshal bskor ba du ma byas//'dab ma stong dang ldan pa yi//dngul gyi me tog tums gtor//bde bar gshegs so rdzogs sangs rgyas//'od zer 
13b3(rgyud): ngo mtshar ldan pa dang //rmad byung chen po'i tshig bshad kyis//zhing rnams rnam par g.yos par gyur//'gro ba'i gti mug mun pa bcom//shes rab mtha' yas gsal 
13b4(rgyud): bar mdzad//bdag kyang snying po 'di yi mthus//phrin las 'di nyid grogs bgyid de//oM hri siM ha nA da hUM phaT//snying po de skad phul nas kyang //thub pa ga la de logs su//
13b5(rgyud): 'khor bcas dga' ba'i tshul gyis ni//pus btsugs thal mo sbyar nas bzhugs//lho'i phyogs kyi zhing khams mchog/grangs med bdun gyi mtha' nyid nas//zhing 
13b6(rgyud): khams rin chen brtsegs pa na///sangs rgyas rin chen dpal bzhugs pa'i//drung du byang chub sems dpa'i gce//spyan ras gzigs dbang yid      bzhin gyi//nor bu 
13b7(rgyud): 'khor lo bsgyur ba che//gser gyi ri bo'i 'dog 'dra ba//byang chub sems tshogs bye ba phrag/bcu dang lhan cig mkha' la gshegs//glog 'gyu bzhin du zhing 'dir byon//
13b8(rgyud): bcom ldan mgon po thub pa la//phyag 'tshal bskor ba du ma byas//'dab ma stong dang ldan pa yi//rin chen gser gyi me tog ni//rgya che du ma 'thor bar byed//bde bar   
14a1(rgyud): @#//gshegs so rdzogs sangs rgyas//'od zer ngo mtshar ldan pa dang //rmad byung che ba'i tshigs bshad kyis//zhing rnams rnam par g.yos par 
14a2(rgyud): gyur//'gro ba'i ser sna 'ching ba bcad//sbyin pa'i 'byung gnas gter chen rdol//bdag kyang snying po 'di'i mthus//phrin las 'di nyid grogs bgyid do//oM pad ma ba 
14a3(rgyud): ra da ke hUM phaT////snying po de skad phul nas kyang //thub pa ga la de logs su//'khor bcas dag pa'i tshul gyis su//pus btsugs thal mo sbyar nas bzhugs//nub kyi   
14a4(rgyud): phyogs kyi zhing khams mchog/grangs med bdun gyi mtha' nyid nas//zhing khams pad ma brtsegs pa na//sangs rgyas pad ma 'byung gnas bzhugs//drung du byang chub 
14a5(rgyud): sems dpa'i gtso//spyan ras gzigs dbang thugs rje can//pad ma gar gyi dbang phyug gtso//pad ma ra ga'i ('dra )'dra ba//byang chub sems tshogs bye ba phrag/bcu dang 
14a6(rgyud): lhan cig mkha' la gshegs//glog 'gyu bzhin du zhing 'dir byon//bcom ldan mgon po thub pa la//phyag 'tshal bskor ba du ma byas//'dab ma stong dang ldan pa'i pad ma ra 
14a7(rgyud): ga'i me tog tog ni//rgya che du ma 'thor bar byed//bde bar gshegs so rdzogs sangs rgyas//'od zer ngo mtshar ldan pa dang //rmad byung chen po'i tshigs bcad kyis//zhing rnam+sa 
14b1(rgyud): rnam par g.yos par gyur//'gro ba'i 'dod chags 'dam rdzab skams//tshul khrims byug spos char du phab//bdag kyang snying po 'di yi mthus//'phrin las 'di nyid grog+sa 
14b2(rgyud): bgyid do//oM pad ma nir te shwa ra hUM phaT//snying po de skad phul nas kyang //thub pa ga la de logs su//'khor bcas dga' ba'i tshul gyis su//pus gtsugs thal mo sbyar 
14b3(rgyud): nas bzhugs//byang gi phyogs kyi zhing khams mchog/grangs med bdun gyi mtha' nyid kyi//zhing khams rdo rje bkod pa na//sangs rgyas rdo rje thogs med bzhugs//
14b4(rgyud): drung du byang chub sems dpa'i gtso//mgon po spyan ras gzigs dbang ni//thugs rje chen po mi bshol ba//rin chen mar gad ri bo 'dra//byang chub sems mchog bye ba 
14b5(rgyud): phrag/bcu dang lhan cig mkha' la gshegs//glog 'gyu bzhin du zhing 'dir byon//bcom ldan mgon po thub pa la//phyag 'tshal bskor ba du ma byas//'dab ma stong

14b7(rgyud): thams cad bsregs//brtson 'grus bskul ba'i me phung 'bar//bdag kyang snying po 'di yi mthus//phrin las 'di nyid grogs bgyid do//oM ra ta na A gra A pra ti ha sta 
15a1(rgyud): hUM paT//snying po de skad phul nas kyang //thub pa ga la de legs su//'khor bcas dga' ba'i tshul gyis ni//pus btsugs thal mo sbyar nas

15a5(rgyud): dang ldan pa yid//rin chen shel gyi me tog ni//rgya che du ma 'ther bar byed//bde bar gshegs so bcom ldan 'das//'od zer ngo mtshar ldan pa dang //rmad byung chen po'i

15b2(rgyud): zhing khams bdud rtsi brtsegs pa na//sangs rgyas sman gyi 'byung gnas bzhugs//drung du byang chub sems dpa'i gtso//mgon po spyan ras gzigs dbang ni//rje btsun lha la

17b1(rgyud): 'gyu bzhin du zhing 'dir byen//bcom ldan mgon po thub pa la//phyag 'tshal bskor ba du byas//'dab ma sdong dang ldan pa yi//an d+ha rnyil gyi me tog ni//rgya che du ma 'thor bar 
17b2(rgyud): byed//bde bar gshegs so rdzogs sangs rgyas//'od zer ngo mtshar ldan pa dang //rmad byung chen po'i tshigs bshad kyis//zhing rnams rnam par g.yos par gyur//'gro ba'i shes

18a1(rgyud): stobs kyis tshar gcod mdzad//lag pa thal sbyar phyed btsums 'byed//sor mo gcig snol gcig 'khyud dang //kun bsnol kun 'khyud kun gdengs dang //
18a2(rgyud): rgyab snol res khyud kun 'khyud dang //sos reg 'khyug sgreng kun sgreng dang //mnyam bzhag dang ni phyag gi rgya//sa gnon skyabs sbyin sogs//phyag rgya du mas bgegs sel mdzad//
18a3(rgyud): gzhan yang 'jig rten skyong ba'i tshogs//ma lus 'khor dang bcas pa rnams//gdong bcu cig pa zhes bya ba'i//bla na med pa'i rig sngags rmad//ngo mtshar 'byung zhes thos ma thag/
18a4(rgyud): lus kyang shin tu 'dar gyur nas//rgyal mchog gtso bo'i drung lhags te//phyag 'tshal thal mo sbyar nas ni//rgyal mchog gtso bo'i drug lhags te//spyan sngar 'dug te 'di skad gsol//
18a5(rgyud): gang zhig gdong bcu gcig pa yi//rig sngags lan cig 'dzin pa dang //rgyun tu 'don dang yid la byed//'jig rten mgon po spyan ras gzigs//dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshogs 
18a6(rgyud): 'di rnams//gang gis yid la byed pa 'am//yang na lan cig dran yang rung //dge ldan rigs kyi bu de ni//phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba rnams kyi sras//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi 
18a7(rgyud): bshes//rig pa'i lha mo rnams kyi yab//byis pa rnams kyi gnyen yin te//bdag cag rnams ni de yi 'bangs//skyes bu khyu mchog chen po ste//bsod nams kun gyi gtar 
18b1(rgyud): ldan zhing //de yi tshul khrims ral ba med//mi de skye ba don yod do//lha rnams dga' nas spa gseb du//ye shes 'od zer bdud rtsi 'dzud//'jig rten 'di yi nad rigs dang //
18b2(rgyud): gdon rigs ji snyed yod pa 'am//rig sngags byad sdems ma lus pas//de la tshugs par mi 'gyur ro//cho ga bzhin du spyad pa yis//tshangs pa'i tshe tshad gnas nas kyang //
18b3(rgyud): shi ba sems brgyal med par ni//gnas 'pho tshul gyis mtho ris 'jug/rgyal ba'i zhing du gshegs nas kyang //rgyal ba rnams la chos nyan cing //rgyal sras rnams dang /
18b4(rgyud): lhan cig tu//stobs dang mi 'jigs kun brnyes te//mtshan dang dpe' byad ldan gyur nas//ska cig glog 'gyu ji bzhin du//'gro ba ma lus dag byed cing //zhing rnams ma
18b5(rgyud): lus dag par byed//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi rgyud rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po pad ma dra ba zhes bya ba las/lha 'byung ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o///
18b6(rgyud): //de nas lag na rdo rje 'dzin//bzhengs te thal sbyar thub pa la//phyag 'tshal phyogs su 'khod nas ni//gsang bas bstod nas 'di skad gsol//phyogs
18b7(rgyud): bcur bzhugs pa'i rgyal dbang po//mgon po spyan ras gzigs dbang kun//rang rang gnas nas gshegs nas byon//'gro la thugs rtse chen po yis//snying po byin brlabs
19a1(rgyud): ldan pa gsol//rmad du byung ba'i snying po la//phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba thams cad dges//rig pa 'dzin rnams mthun par dges//'on kyang 'gro mang 
19a2(rgyud): bzung ba dang //dngos grub thams cad myur ba dang //snying po mtha dang ldan pa'i phyir//mgon po thugs rje che rnams kyis//byin bslabs ldan pa rtsa ba'i sngags//'dren pa de ring 
19a3(rgyud): bshad du gsol//rig sngags nyi ma zla ba'i 'od//rtsa ba'i sngags kyis sprin bral byed//'gro la thugs rtse mdzad pa dang //bdag la phan pa'i don slad du//rgyal ba mya ngan kun 
19a4(rgyud): bral bas//rtsa ba'i gzungs sngags bshad du gsol//de nas bcom ldan thub pa ches//sdig kun myur du sgrol bya yi//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//sku gsung 
19a5(rgyud): thugs kyi rdo rje las//spyan ras gzigs dbang seng ge'i sgra'i//rtsa ba'i rig sngags 'di gsungs so////na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na ma:Ar+ya ba lo ki te shwa ra ya/bo ti d+hi sa twA ya/
19a6(rgyud): ma hA sa twA ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/ta d+ya thA/oM a ka Te/b+hi ka Te/tri ka Te/ka Ta nga ka Te/ka To bIr+ya swAhA//yon tan ye shes tshogs rnams kun//rig sngags 'di ni nges par 
19a7(rgyud): bskyed//khyad par du ni 'dze nad la//rtsa las byung ba'i me bzhin no//rtog ldan rnam shes brgyad kyi bzhi//sbyor phyir maN+Dal ltam pa brgyad//ser skya ba yi bdud rtsis byug/
19b1(rgyud): yan lag bcu gnyis 'khor bcas pa'i//sngags grangs byin gyis brlabs byug nas//lhag ma yan lag thig ler bya//cung zad lce las myangs pas ni//dus dus sum brgya drug
19b2(rgyud): bcu'i tshad//nyi ma grangs kyis dag nas ni//lo gcig dus su rtogs ldan 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan thub pa ches//dpal bskyed 'chi ba bzlog pa yi//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms
19b3(rgyud): zhugs nas//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje las//mgon po yid bzhin 'khor lo yi//rtsa ba'i rigs sngags 'di gsungs so//na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na ma:Ar+ya a ba lo ki te
19b4(rgyud): shwa ra ya/bo d+hi satwa ya/ma hA satwa ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/ta d+ya thA/oM tsakra wa rti tsin d+ha ma Ni/ma hA pad me ru ru tiSh+Tha dz+wa la a kar sha ya hri:phaT swAhA/'jig rten 'jig rten 'das
19b5(rgyud): ba yi//blo bur gnod pa bsal ba'i phyir//bdun phrag sngags dang mnyam pa yi//yungs dkar ser po brgya rtsa brgyad//rang gi lus sam yan lag la//mchod dang bcas
19b6(rgyud): pas bdags par bya//de la yang yang gsol ba gdab//de nas bcom ldan thubapa ches//khams gsum dbang du bya ba yi//ting nge 'dzin la snyems (bzhugs)nas//sku gsung thugs
19b7(rgyud): kyi rdo rje las//mgon po pad ma gar dbang gi//rtsa ba'i sngags 'di gsungs so////na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na ma:Ar+yA pa lo gi te shwa ra ya/bo d+hi sa twA ya//ma hA sa twA ya/
20a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/ta d+ya thA/na ma sarbas satwA bA swA na d+ha ni/na ma sarbas satwA pa se na/a ha ra ni/na ma sarbas satwAb+ha yod dwa ra Na yA/na ma sarbas 
20a2(rgyud): satwA pA pa pra sha ma na ya/na ma sarbas twA d+hi tsita gin nA sha na ka ra ya/na ma sarbas twA ban d+ha na che da na ka ra yA/na ma sarbas satwA du/kha bi mo k+Sha na ka ra yA/na ma sarba sa twA an+d+ha 
20a3(rgyud): ka ra bi d+ha ma na ka ra yA/na ma sa rbas satwA bad+yI dwA ra rA dza pra sha ma na ye/ma ha yo gi shwa ra sa hA sra ta s+ya na/skri tam i daM Ar+yA ba lo ki te sha ra mu khyod gi NAM/rI Sha bi bi d+ha ra//
20a4(rgyud): dA+e ba/nA ga/ya k+Sha ra k+Sha sa/sha kra bra h+ma lo ka pA la/b+hiSh+N+ya ma hA+e shwa ra/nA ra ya na kan/ku bA+ai ra/a su ren tra/mA tri ga na/na ma skri tan badzra khU ra hri da yan/ha ya g+h+ri ba na ma pa 
20a5(rgyud): ra ma hri na yan/a warta ya Sh+ya mi/a pa pra me yod d+ha sa d+ha kan/a b+ha ha na/sarba pu tra nan/sarba bi na ya kaM a mu kaM/sarba karma bi sha nan tsa/bi na ya kan/ta d+ya thA/oM hUM hUM hUM/
20a6(rgyud): tra ru la/ta ru la/bi ru la/sarba bi Sha g+ha ta ka/sarba b+h+ya ta bi d+ha ra ni/pra dza li ta/a na la/bi spu ling+ga aT+Ta T+Ta hA sa/ke swa ra y+wa ra y+wa sa Ta/a To pa/TaM ka ra ya/badzra khu ra na ri g+ha ta na/
20a7(rgyud): tsa li ta ba su d+ha ta la/ni sho sa ma ru tod k+Shi pa ta d+ha ra ni/badzra to sho si te ti/si ta ma du to//ko Ta pra sha ma na ka ra/ba ra du Sh+TaM bad+yi karma na saM b+ha na ka ra/sa bid to pa tra te sha kar /pa ra 
20b1(rgyud): ma shan ti ka ra/bud d+ha b+ho ya mi te/b+ha ga wa na ha ya g+hi ba/hri dwa yA/ma swarta ye Sh+ya mi/khA da khA da/ma hA re tra naM maN+ta na/rak+Sha aT+Ta ha sa ti tan+ta mAn tra na/sid d+he sid d+he/sarba karma Sha 
20b2(rgyud): me|sid d+he sid d+he/de hi de hi/a bai sha ya/a bai sha ya/pra be sha ya pra be sha ya/sarba dz+wa ra bi shA tsA na/sarba gra hwe Sha a pra ti ha sta b+ha ba/d+hu na d+hu na/bi d+hu na bi d+hu na/ma tha ma tha/
20b3(rgyud): pra ma tha/sarba par kra ma kri ta na da gra/ka khor ta dur laM gri ta te/mu shi ka pi sha ka ra/b+ha sha daMSh+Ta/bi sha tsen yo ga a bi tsa ra/ba shaM ka ra na/sid d+ha an dza na/tsa gi mo ha na/tsid 
20b4(rgyud): Ta big ni pa na ka ra/nid d+h+ya a par bre sha ka na/trA sa ya trA sa ya/ma hA bo d+hi swAhA/na rin d+ha di sha nan/sarba b+ha ye b+h+ya/sarba satwA nan/rak+Sha rak+Sha maM/bud b+ha rma saM/g+ha a nu dz+nyA 
20b5(rgyud): taM me karma shi g+h+raM/ku ru ku ru phaT/ha ma g+h+ri ba phaT/badzra daM Sh+Tod ka Ta b+ha ye b+ha ra pa na phaT/pA ra man+ta nam nA sha na ka ra ya phaT/ba ra du Sh+tha bad+yi ka na saM b+ha k+Sha na kar ya phaT/sarba gra hod ts+tshe da 
20b6(rgyud): ye phaT/sarba gra he Sh+ya a pra ti ha sta ya phaT/bA ra ha mu kha ya phaT/ye ken tsid ma mA a hi shi na ba ka ya/dra mi Ta/man+t+ra yan+ta ma man ti/dza ba ya ti dzu han ti/ka khor d+ha kur wan ti  
20b7(rgyud): nan/sarba dz+wa ra mu khen pra krid ta ya phaT/na ma sarba duSh+TA gra hod swa da na ya/ha ya g+h+ri ba sid d+han du me man+t+ra pa da ni swAhA/oM a mi tod b+ha ya hUM hUM phaT phaT swAhA/oM na mo ha ya ye swAhA//
21a1(rgyud): @#/ O /oM na ma sa mud ye swAhA//na ma sa twa nany+tsa sid d+h+yan tu me man+t+ra pa dA ni swAhA//gang zhig klus non mi la ni//dung gi mu tig sngags stong gis//byin gyis brlabs 
21a2(rgyud): nas gu gul bdug/lus la thogs na legs par 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan thub pa ches//thogs med sems dpa' zhes bya ba'i//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs nas//sku 
21a3(rgyud): gsung thugs kyi rdo rje las//mgon po snying rje mi bshol ba'i//rtsa ba'i rig sngags 'di gsungs so//na mo ran t+na tra ya yA/na ma/Ar+ya pa lo ki te shwa ra yA/bo d+hi sa twA ya/
21a4(rgyud): ma hA satwA yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/ta d+ya thA/na ma sarba pa tra mo tsa ka raM/sarba rA+o ga pra sha ma naM/sarba b+hu ta ni b+ha ya ka raM/a sh+Ta k+Ta na pan Ti sa ta yA/ta ra ka samyaka ta ra/na ma dwa shwa 
21a5(rgyud): dag gi b+hu ta yA te b+h+yo//na ma/samyaka saM bud d+ha ke Ti nan/a he/na ma saM skri ta/Ar+ya pa lo ki te shA ra bshad te d+ha rA ni/ma hA ka ru Ni kA ya/A gra b+ha b+hi She ti/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/a  
21a6(rgyud): grA na mdza la pa ti kA ra/ma hA ka ru na b+hu ba/sa man ta ha ran tu/ta d+ya thA/oM dza Ta dza Ta/dzi Ti dzi Ti/dzu T+ya dzu T+ya/ta ra ta ra/ti ri ti ri/tu ru tu ru/ka la ka la/ki li ki li/ku lu ku lu/
21a7(rgyud): ma hA ka ru ni ka ya/sarba ta thA ga ta ma hA swa saM/gra hA/a nu saM sarba hA ran tu/e h+ya hi/sid d+ha ya/e h+ya hi/tsa Ta tsa Ta/tsi Ti tsi Ti/tsu T+ya tsu T+ya/sarba bo d+hi satwA pra ni d+ha na b+ha ne/
21b1(rgyud): a k+Sha ra ma nu san d+ha yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/sa b+hu be/e h+ye hi/ra la ra la/ri li ri li/ru lu ru lu/d+h+ya na ngi mok+Sha sa ma d+hi/sa ma ba ti b+his tsa ra/e tre sh+Ta/tsa la tsa la/ci li 
21b2(rgyud): tsi li/tsu lu tsu lu/bo d+hi bo d+hi/e hi i hi/bud d+ha bud d+ha/bud d+ha sad+ya na/d+yarmasat+ya na/o Ti swo Ti sgra/sarba ma hA mA+ai tri bud d+ha ni ba ra na ru//ma hA ka ru na me g+hen/sarba rA ga ni ba ra ya 
21b3(rgyud): ni ru/bu na bu na/bi bu na bi bu na/ma tha ma tha/pra ma tha pra ma tha/a Ta s+tho Ta s+tha/e h+ya hi o h+yi/ma mA sarba sha pu man tu karma bi sho d+ha ya//ma hA ka ru ni ka yA/ma hA pad ma hA sta ya/
21b4(rgyud): pad ma a sa na ya swAhA//sha ta ad nya u pa b+hi d+ha ya swAhA//ku ru ti Ni swAhA/shU la nye swAhA/tsa Ta naM swAhA/badzra Ni swAhA/tre ne tra ya swAhA/bra ha ru pa ya swAhA/arga ya swAtA/
21b5(rgyud): sha shi na ya swAhA/pra tsa sa Ta ye swAhA/ya me ye swAhA/an ta tse bi shud d+he swAhA/dze ra u de bya b+hu te b+h+ya swAhA/swaM pa tse b+h+ya b+hu te b+h+ya swA/a du pa du ke b+h+yo d+haM b+h+ya swAhA/bi di ka b+h+yo b+he hi b+h+ye   
21b6(rgyud): swAhA/bai ha de b+h+yo b+he d+hi b+h+ya swAhA/sa ni pa ri ke b+h+yo b+h+ya swAhA/sarba pu te b+h+yo b+h+ya swAhA/ni mi in ti ri ka sha tsa re b+h+yo b+h+ya swAhA/ta d+ya thA/oM ta Ta ta Ta/ti Ti ti Ti/tu T+ya tu T+ya/na ma sarba  
21b7(rgyud): b+ha ye b+h+ya/sarba u pa dra bya b+h+ya/rak+Sha rak+Sha ku ru ku ru swAhA/gra sa gra sa/ma thA ma thA/pra ma thA pra ma thA/ha na ha na/da ha da ha/pa tsa pa tsa/na ma Ar+ya pa lo ki te shwa ra yA/bo ti  
22a1(rgyud): @#/ O /satwA ya/ma hA sa twA ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/an dz+nyA pa ya ti swAhA////shang kar yi sa bon la//stong du bzlas nas dri chu dang /lhan cig skrangs 
22a2(rgyud): la byugs na ni/lhog nad drag po 'jom par byed//de nas bcom ldan thub pa ches/rig sngags byed stong gnod pa yi//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs nas//sku gsung thugs 
22a3(rgyud): kyi rdo rje las//mgon po don yod zhags pa yi//tsa ba'i rig sngags 'di gsungs so//nam ste yang d+he a nu ga da/pra ti Sh+Ti te b+h+ya/sarba bud d+ha bo d+hi satwe b+h+ya/na ma pra t+ye ka bud d+ha/
22a4(rgyud): Ar+yA ba ka saM g+he b+h+yo//a ti tA a nag ta phra t+yad pan+ne b+h+yo//na ma samyaka ga ta naM/na ma samyaka pra ti pan nA naM/na ma shwa ra dwa ti pu tri ya/na mo ma hA ma tA ye/nam/Ar+ya mA+ai tri ya pra mu khe 
22a5(rgyud): b+h+yo//mahA bo satwA b+h+ya/na ma su pra ti Sh+Tha te she len+d+ra rA dza pra mu khe b+h+yo//na ma sarba ta thA ga te b+h+yo//ar had b+h+ya//samyaka sam bud d+he b+h+yo//b+ha ga wa te b+h+ye/na ma su bar+Na bar+Na su pra ti b+ha di nar de te rA dzA 
22a6(rgyud): yA|ta thA ga ta ya/na ma si ha bi kri Di ta rA dza ya ta thA ga ta ya/na mo a mi ta b+ha ya ta thA ga ta ya/nam su pra ti Sh+Thi ta ma ni ku Ta rA dza ya ta tha ga ta ya/nam sa man+ta ra smyud ga ta na shrI ku Ta rA dza ya ta 
22a7(rgyud): tha ga ta ya/na mo byi pa sh+yi ni ta thA ga ta ya/na mo bi shwa b+hu be ta thA ga ta ya/na ma kra ku tshu na d+ha ya ta thA ga ta yA/na ma ka na ka mu na ye ta thA ga ta yA/nam ga sh+ya pa ya ta thA ge ta ya/na mo shAkya mu na ye ta 
22b1(rgyud): thA ga ta ya/ar ra ha he saM myaka saM bud d+ha ya/tad+ya thA/oM mu ni mu ni ma hA mu na ye swAhA/oM sha me sha me ma hA sha me/rak+Sha rak+Sha maM sarba satwA nany+tsa/sarba pA pa pra sha ma na ye swAhA/na ma su ba ri kirti 
22b2(rgyud): ta na ma d+he ya shrI ye ta thA ga ta ya/na ma sa man+ta a wa b+ha sa bi dzi ta saM gra ma shrI ye ta ma ta ya/na ma rat+na pra b+ha sa sha ra ra dza ya/ta thA ga ta ya/na ma a pra ti ha sta b+he Sha dz+ye ra dza ya/ta thA ga ta a/na mo bi 
22b3(rgyud): kran ta ga mi ni/ta thA ga ta ya/na mo bud d+ha ya/na mo d+harma ya/na ma sa g+ha ya//na me a ti Sha a nu ga ta pra t+ya pan+ma b+h+ye/bud d+he d+h+ya/b+ha ga wa te b+h+ya/ta d+ya thA/smri ti bar d+ha ni/ma ti bar d+ha ni/ga ti 
22b4(rgyud): bar d+ha ni/d+h+ri ti bar d+ha ni/pra dz+nyA bar d+ha ni/pra ti b+hA naM bar b+ha ni/d+h+ye na brda ni/sa marga par d+ha ni/bi pa shI na bar d+ha ni/sarba bo d+hi pa k+Sha d+ha rma bar d+ha ni/sa kA la bud d+ha d+harma pa ri pu ra Ni ye swAhA///
22b5(rgyud): na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na ma Ar+yA a ba lo ki te sha ra yA/be d+hi satwa ya/ma hA satwA ya/mahA ka ru Ni ka ya/e b+h+ye na ma skri twa i daM Ar+yA pa lo ki tai shwa ra mu khod gi r+NaM/a mo g+ha bA sha rA dza na ma 
22b6(rgyud): hrid yaM/ta thA ga staM mu kha b+ha Shi taM ma hI ta/par Sha na ma d+he a ho i daM ni a brta ya khyu mi sid d+h+yan tu me man+t+ra pa de/sarba kAr+ya ni sarba satwA nany+tsa rak+Sha b+ha wa n+tu/ta d+ya thA/oM tsa ra tsa ra/tsi tsi tsi ri/
22b7(rgyud): tsu ru tsu ru/ma ra ma ra/mi ri mi ri/mu ru mu ru/ma hA ka ru Ni kaM/sa ra sa ra/si ri si ri/su ru su ru/dzu ru dzu ru/dzi ri dzi ri/bi ri bi ri/pi ri pi ri/mi ri mi ri/ma hA pad ma ha sta/
23a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ka la ka la/ki li ki li/ku lu ku lu/ma hA shud d+ha satwA/bud d+ha bud d+ha/bo d+h+ya bo d+h+ya/bo d+h+yi bo d+h+yi/bo d+ha ya bo d+ha ya/ka Na ka Na/ki Ni ki Ni/ku N+ya ku N+ya/
23a2(rgyud): pa ra ma shud d+ha satwA/ka ra ka ra/ki ri ki ri/ku ru ku ru/ma hA s+tha ma pra pa ta/tsa la tsa la/saM tsa la saM tsa la/bi tsa la/bi tsa la/pra tsa la pra tsa la/e ta Ta Ta e Ta Ta/b+ha ra b+ha ra/b+hi ri 
23a3(rgyud): b+hi ri/b+hu ru b+hu ru/ta ra ta ra/ti ri ti ri/tu ru tu ru /e h+ye hi/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/ma hA pA shu pa ti bA+e sha d+ha ra/d+ha ra d+ha ra/d+hi ri d+hi ri/d+hu ru d+hu ru/ta ra ta ra/sa ra sa ra/tsa ra tsa ra/pa ra 
23a4(rgyud): pa ra/ba ra ba ra/ma ra ma ra/lar la ra/ha ra ha ra/hA hA/hI hI/hU hU/oM ka ra ka ra bra h+ma bA+e sha d+ha ra/d+ha ra ng+ha ra/d+hi ri d+hi ri/ng+hu ru d+hu ru/ta ra ta ra /sa ra sa ra/tsa ra tsar/b+ha ra b+ha ra/na ra na ra/
23a5(rgyud): ra Sh+mi sha ta sra pra ti maN Ti ta shA rI raM/dz+wA la dz+wA la/ta pa ta pa/ta ma ta ma/da ma da ma/b+ha sa b+ha sa/b+h+ra ma b+h+ra ma/b+ha ga wan/sA+o ma/a d+yed te/ya ma/ba ru na/ku bA+ai ra/bra h+ma in+d+ra/bA 
23a6(rgyud): yU/ak+ni/d+ha na dA/de ba rI Sha gi Na/b+h+rar ci t+ta tsa ra na/su ru su ru ru/tsu ru tsu ru/mu ru mu ru/g+hu ru g+hu ru/sA nad ku ma ra/ru dra b+hA sa ba/b+hi Sh+Tha/d+ha na da/bA yu/ag ni/rI Shi/na ya 
23a7(rgyud): ke༐ bi na ya ka/ba hu bi bi d+ha bA+e sha d+ha ra/d+ha rad+ha ra/d+hi ri d+hi ri/d+hu ru d+hu ru/ta ra tar/tha ra tha ra/g+ha ra g+ha ra/ya ra ya ra/la ra la ra/ha ra ha ra/pa ra pa ra/sa ra sa ra/ba ra ba ra/d+ha ra d+ha ra/
23b1(rgyud): ba ra dwa ya ka/sa man+ta a wa lo ki te/bi lo ki ti/bi lo ki ti/lo ki shwa ra/ma hA+e shwa ra/tri b+hu ba ne shwa ra/sarba gu na sa ma/a laM/kri ta/a ba lo ki te shwa ra na/mu hu mu hu/mu ru mu ru/
23b2(rgyud): mu ya mu ya/mu ny+tsa mu ny+tsa/rak+Sha rak+Sha saM sarba satwa nany+tsa/sarba b+ha ye b+ha/sarbe pa sar ge b+ha/sarba bA d+hi b+ha/sarba bi She b+ha/sarba dz+wa re b+ha/sarba gra he b+h+ya/e baM bAn ta ban d+ha na/ta rdza kha na/
23b3(rgyud): ta Da ka na/rA dza na/stas ka ra/a k+ni/u dA ka/bI Sha/shas+t+ra pa ri mo tsa ka/ka Na ka Na/ki Ni ki Ni/ku N+ya ku N+ya/tsa ra tsa ra/tsi Shi tsi ri/tsu ru tsu ru/in+d+ri ya/bA la bo d+hI ang ga/
23b4(rgyud): tsA tur Ar+yA saM pra ka sha ka/ta pa ta pa/ta ma ta ma/da ma da ma/sa ma sa ma/ma sa ma sa/d+ha ma d+ha ma/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/ma hA ta mo an d+ha ka ra bi d+ha ma na/Sha Ta pa ra mi ta pa ri pU ra ka/
23b5(rgyud): ma la ma la/mi li mi li/mu lu mu lu/Ta Ta Ta Ta/Tha Tha Tha Tha/Ti Ti Ti Ti/Tu Tu Tu Tu/Thi Thi Thi Thi/Sh+ya Th+ya Th+ya Th+ya/e N+ye ya tsa rma kri ta pa ri ka ra e h+ya hi//ma hA ka ru Ni ka/i sha ra/ma hA+e 
23b6(rgyud): shar/ma hA b+hu ta gA Na saM b+ha dza ka/ka ra ka ra/ki ri ki ri/ku ru ku ru/pa ra pa ra/ha ra ha ra/ba ra ba ra/sa ra sa ra/ta ra ta ra/tsa ra tsa ra/ka Ta ka Ta/ki Ti ki Ti/ku Tu ku Tu/ma Ta ma Ta/ma ha 
23b7(rgyud): bi shud d+ha bI Sha yan b+ha si na/ma hA ka ru Ni ka shwe ta yadz+nyo pa bi ta/rat+na ma ku Ta Ta mA la d+ha ra sarba dz+nyA shi ra si kri ta/dza Ta ma ku Ta ma hAd b+hu ta ka ma la a laM kri tra ka ka tA la/d+ha na sa ma d+hi bi mok+Sha/
24a1(rgyud): @#/ O /a pra kam pya bA hu sa ta san/ma hA ka ru Ni ka shwe ta yadz+nyA+o pa bi ta/rat+na ma kuT Ta mA la d+hA ra sarba dz+nyA shi ra si kri ta/dza Ta ma ku Ta ma hAd b+hu ta ka ma la a laM kri ta ka ra 
24a2(rgyud): tA la/d+h+ya na sa ma d+h+yi bi mok+Sha/a pra kam pya b+ha hu sa ta san ta ti pa rI pa tsa ra ka/ma hA ru nI ka/sarba karma a wa ra Na bi sho d+ha ka/sarba dz+nyA dz+nyA na pa ri pU ra ka/sarba byA d+hi ba ri mo tsa ka/sarba satwA 
24a3(rgyud): a sha pa ri pU ra ka/sarba sa twA mA shwa sa yan+tA ka ra na mo stu te swAhA//a mo g+ha ya swAhA/a mo g+ha pA sha ya swAhA/a dzi ta ya swAhA/a pa ra dzi ta ya swAhA/a mi ta b+hA ya swAhA/a mi ta b+ta su 
24a4(rgyud): ta ya swAhA/mA ra s+ya na pra marda na yA+e swAhA/a b+ha ye swAhA/a b+ha ya pra dA ya swAhA/dza ye yA swAhA/bidza ye yA swAhA/dza ye bi dza ye yA swAhA/ba ra dwa ya swAhA/ba ra pra dA ya swAhA/a kA la mri d+yu pa sha 
24a5(rgyud): man ye swAhA/i dam tsarma karma ku ru na mo stu ste swAhA/oM ra Na ra Na hUM phaT swAhA/oM dza ya hUM phaT swAhA/eM dzri dzri dzuM swAhA/oM hUM dza ya swAhA/oM hriHtrai lo kya bi dza ya/a mo g+ha pA shA a pra ti hA sti hri :
24a6(rgyud): ha hUM phaT swAhA//oM pA shu pa ti swAhA//oM A ro li ka swAhA/oM ba hu le ba hu le swAhA/oM A ro lig hri hri hUM phaT swAhA/ni ya ta a ni ya ta be ta ni ya/a shu b+ha se me b+ha ga wan/sarba karma a  
24a7(rgyud): a she Sha ta k+Sha kaM ku ru swAhA/oM bud ma ha sta ya swAhA/oM d+hud d+ha d+harma saM g+ha ya swAhA////rabs chad thod par ro bsregs sa//yungs dkar ser po dri chur bcas/bskyed de myu gu sngags stong brjod//
24b1(rgyud): 'bras bu la ni khri brjod bya//lus la thogs na byad sdems sogs//nad dang gdon dag myur du sel//gzhan dag las rnams gang la yang //bdun bdun sngags kyi grangs kyis sbyar//
24b2(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan thub pa ches//ltos 'gro las ni rab rgyal ba'i//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs nas//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje las//mgon po ha la ha la yi//rtsa ba'i bsang sngag+sa 
24b3(rgyud): 'di gsungs so////na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na maHsarba bud d+ha be d+hi satwA/su ru sha ri Shi ya/na ma Ar+yA a ba lo ki te shwa ra ya/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA satwA ya/ma hA ku ru Ni ka ya/na mo ma hA s+tha 
24b4(rgyud): ma pra pa ta/Ar+yA mA+e tri ya/Ar+ya many+dzu shri Ar+ya badzra pA ni/a d+hi kaM/bo d+hi satwA/ma hA satwA b+h+ya/i daM na ma skri te/b+ha ga wan/Ar+ya a wa lo ki te shwa ra taM/hri da ya sarba karma sa daM/sarba gra hod  
24b5(rgyud): ts+tshe da na ka raM//b+haM sa a ba ra Na bi nA ka ra/ba shi taM/saM ya s+thi taM/he bo d+hi satwA/ma hA bo d+hi satwA/pri ya bo d+hi swA tra/he ha la le/Ar+ya a ba lo ki te shwA ra ya/ba ra ma mA+e tre tsa ti ka ru Ni ka/
24b6(rgyud): a pra ti ha sta tsi ta ka ru Ni kaM/ku ru ku ru/rak+Sha maM/mang g+ha ma/bi haM g+ha ma/birdza yAn te/ba dza yan te/ma hA yan te/d+hu ru d+hu ru/d+ha ra d+ha ra/tsa yan+t+ra tsa man+t+ra/na mi me h+yi d+hi/sarba satwA 
24b7(rgyud): b+hA ya pra ha ra Na ya/ha ra ha ra/hi b+ha h+ye nod b+ha wa/bA bA bA bA/b+ha ba na ga ku ru ku ru/tu ru tu ru/g+hu ru g+hu ru/su ru su ru/mu ru mu ru/tsu ru tsu ru/sre pra sA+e d+ha/sa ta ma bi ma la/pha 
25a1(rgyud): @#/ O /la pha la mUr te/Ar+yA pa lo ki ti shwa ra yA/pA ra ma mA+e tri ka ru Ni ka/kri Sh+NA dzi na/na d+ha badzra ma ku Ta bi lam b+ha//pra lam b+ha/tsa la tsa la/ma laM b+ha la/ka 
25a2(rgyud): la ka la/ha la ha la/ga la ga la/ta la ta la/da la da la/na la na la/tu lu tu lu/hu lu hu lu/hi li hi li/he bo d+hi satwA swAhA/na ma ste ste u/a hUM hUM hUM hri:ku ru ku ru/hri da ya 
25a3(rgyud): a nu sma ra/dza ti ma nu sma ra/i daM me ka rma ku ru ma no ra tha me/pa ri pU ra ya si b+h+yen tu man+t+ra pa dA ni swAhA/snga bar khrus byas gos gtsang bgo/bcom ldan spyan sngar nyin re bzhin//
25a4(rgyud): ba lci sa la ma lhung bas//dkyil 'khor gru bzhi ltam pa ni/bcu gsum bzlas nas byug par bya//lhag mas nad rnams thams cad la//byugs pa tsam gyis sos par nges//
25a5(rgyud): zla ba drug gam bdun rnams zhi//'dod pa thams cad skong 'gyur te//mtshams med lnga byas mis kyang 'grub//gal te grub par ma gyur na//bdag gis mtshams med las 
25a6(rgyud): byas te//dus gsum sangs rgyas kun bslus yin//snying rjes snying rje can 'grub 'gyur//sdang bas khro ba 'grub 'gyur te//rtag pa'i gnas 'dod blo gros can//rnam par mi 
25a7(rgyud): rtog btang snyoms kyis//'dod pa kun 'grub zhen pas min//yid la byed pas 'chi bar 'gyur//se'u bo che'i lcug ma la//ro bsregs thal bas khri phrag bdun//bsngags nas gtor 
25b1(rgyud): bas char ches dang //ser ba myur du chad par byed//dri chu gla rtsi bcas pas ni//sbrul gyi gdug pa sel bar byed//lto 'phye'i rigs la sbrang rgod sogs//'jigs pa rnams la g+hi haM 'phyar//de nas 
25b2(rgyud): bcom ldan thub pa ches//shes rab rnam grol zhes bya ba'i//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs nas//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje las//mgon po pad ma rgyan lung gi/rtsa ba'i rig sngag+sa 
25b3(rgyud): 'di gsungs so////na mo rad na tra yA/na ma Ar+ya pa lo kyi te shwa ra yA/bo d+hi satwa ya/ma hA satwa ya/mahA ka ru Ni ka yA/tad+ya thA/na mo a mi ta b+ha ya ta thA ga ta yA//a ra ha te b+h+ye 
25b4(rgyud): saM myaka saM bud d+ha yA/na maHmahA s+tha ma pra b+ha d+h+ra yA/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA satwA ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/na ma bi b+hu ma/bi ma Na/su pra ti Sha ya/a saM khye ra ya/sur+ya sha ta yaM/e 
25b5(rgyud): saM khye ya/sur+ya sha ta sa hA sra a ti re ka/a b+ha ba ti mUr ta ye/ma hA ma Ni me ku Ta ku Na Da yi/a lam ka ra d+ha ra ni/b+ha ga ba te/pad ma pA na ye/sarba po ka la ya bra shA ma na  
25b6(rgyud): ya/bi ba d+ha mu kha sa ma se she a b+ha sha/sarba satwA pa ri pa tsa ka ya/tad+ya thA/oM b+hur b+hu ba swAhA/ma hA lo ka shwa ra na/nam s+thi mi ra b+ha tra la b+bi nA sha ka/rA ga dwe sha mo hA dz+wa le shwa 
25b7(rgyud): mag sha sa ka/sa ma sha na ka ra rak+Sha ka/sarba a ba ya dur ga ti pra shA ma na ka rA/sarba a sha pa ri pU ra ka/sarba ta thA ga ta sa ma b+ha d+ha na ka rA/e h+ye ma hA bo d+hi satwA pa ra dA/pad mA a lo 
26a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ka saM bud d+ha/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/dza Ta ma ku Ta a laM kri ta sha ri ra Sha ni ma Sha/ka na ka rA dza/badzra bai dur+ya a laM kri ta sha rI raM//ma hA ta dzi na ka ma la a la kri 
26a2(rgyud): ta/bra b+ha ra shi sha ta sa hA sa/a bi la sha ta ka ya/ma hA bo d+hi satwA ba ra dA/bi da ma bi da ma/bi na sha ya bi na sha ya/ma hA ya na tra ma klA+ai sha ka pa ta/a ba bad d+ha d+hi bo d+hi satwo/g+ha ra tsa ra 
26a3(rgyud): ka/pra ma tha na ka rA/pu ru Sha pad+ma/pu ru Sha sa si haM/bI ra bI ra/dzasu tan ta Ta bA ri brid ta/d+ha ma d+ha ma/sa ma sa ma/d+hu ru d+hu ru/pra sha sa ya pra sha sa ya/gi ri gi ri/b+hi bi ri b+hi bi ri/
26a4(rgyud): tsi ri tsi ri/si ri si ri/su ru su ru/d+hi ri d+hi ri/bi d+hi ri bi d+hi ri/ba ri ba ri/mu ru mu ru/mu kha mu kha/mu ya mu ya/mun tsa mun tsa/d+hu na d+hu na/bi d+hu na bi d+hu na/d+hu ru d+hu ru/ga ya ga ya/g+ha ta 
26a5(rgyud): ya g+ha ta ya/ha sa ha sa/pra ha sa pra ha sa/na ga bi si d+ha/ki sha be sa na/ha ra ha ra/saM ha ra saM ha ra/du ru du ru/d+hu ma ha maM gA la/ka la na sha ta pra be ka/a bi b+hi sa ta/b+hi bi da ba sA na/sha 
26a6(rgyud): ma ka sha ma ka/ra ga ka/ma hA bo d+hi satwA/b+ha ra b+ha ra swAhA//thug po che dang shu dag la/khri phrag bzlas nas 'thungs pa ni//shes rab chen po 'phel bar byed//don la mthun pa'i sman dang  
26a7(rgyud): sngar/pad ma'i rtsa ba ka rar bcas//nyi shu rtsa cig yang na bdun//sngags brjod snga bar min par bya//des kyang dbyangs snyan shes rab 'phel//de nas bcom ldan thub pa ches//
26b1(rgyud): dgong pa bla na med ces pa'i//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs nas//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje las/mgon po rdo rje chos chen po'i//rtsa ba'i rig sngags 'di gsungs so////na 
26b2(rgyud): mo rat+na tra ya yA/na ma Ar+ya a ba lo ki te shwa ra yA/bo d+hi satwo ya/ma hA satwA ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/tad ya thA/na mo b+ha ga wa te/bA+ai ro tsa na pra b+ha rA dza ya/ta thA ga ta yA/a mya ka saM 
26b3(rgyud): bud d+ha yA/sarba adz+nya yA/an ta ka ra/ma ma yi tre/tad+ya thA/sA d+ha ni/sA d+ha ni/sid d+ha d+ha ra/ba gi ba gi ba ram sa ra/kle sha kle na de bi na/adz+nya sha shA ya sa/a r+ha t+ya b+h+ye 
26b4(rgyud): sarba pA pa bi shud d+he/d+ha ma ni d+ha ma na/a laM dz+nya na go tsa re/a sa na dz+nya na go tsa ri ni ba shaM sa mun ti/su ba ra pra te/sarba kle sha/d+he sa ni/bi d+ha ma ni/bi d+ha ma ni/bin sha bi na sha/bi na sha ya bi 
26b5(rgyud): na sha ya/b+ha ga wa ti/su ra sa ri ma la ba ti/sa ra sa ra/si ri si ri/su ru su ru/pra sa ra pra sa ra/bi sa ra bi sa ra/a dza ma o ti sa hA sra nan/tsi nan ka ra ya/a pa ra na k+Shi pa ya/
26b6(rgyud): k+Sha ba ma ti b+hu d+ha bi sha yA/ba sha ba ti ni pa ri mi ta dz+nya na go tsa re/bi sha sha ba ti/a sha sha ba ti/a sha sha ma li ni ka sha na go tsa re/ma hA dz+nya a b+hi She ka ma ti su bi ni shrid ta d+ha/he he b+ha 
26b7(rgyud): ga wan ma hA ka ru Ni ka/ku ru ku ra bi sho d+ha ya man/ma mA sarba satwa na ny+tsa ma hA ka ru nod sa ke na/san tsi na tsa ya/saM b+ha ra ta ya saM b+ha ra bra ro ha ya me/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/tsi tsa 
27a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ma pa ri shud d+ha yA/a mu ka bi ku ru bi dza na Na/sa ra sa ra me b+ha ga ba ti ma ma na/bo d+hi tsi t+taM sa b+hu de ma san du ne/bi d+hi mi ri ka ra ni/bud d+ha bo d+hi pra he ni/ra 
27a2(rgyud): ga pra ha ta r+ya ka ga ti/du kho gu la ni/de ba ma nu Sh+ya ga ti/saM pa ti ni bi ka to//kle sha sarba nu d+ha d+ha rma/sid d+han tu me man+t+ra pA da ni swAhA////snga bar langs nas khrus byas te/dkyil 
27a3(rgyud): dkrung mnyam bzhag phyag rgya can//yid kyi sa phyag 'tshal nyi shu gcig /rdo rje chos la dad pas brjod//brgyad dang bcu bzhi bco lnga la//gos (ga)sar gon nas gso sbyong blang //
27a4(rgyud): me tog can dan dkar sogs mchod//don grub sdig sel shes rab 'phel//de nas bcom ldan thub pa ches//bdud rnams 'joms pa rdo rje yi//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs 
27a5(rgyud): nas//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje las//ha ri zhon pa chen po yi/rtsa ba'i rig sngags 'di gsungs so//na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na maHAr+yA a ba pa lo ki te shwa ra yA/bo d+hi satwa yA/
27a6(rgyud): ma hA satwA yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/tad+ya thA/na mo b+ha ga wa te/a mi ta b+ha ya/ta thA ga ya/ar hA te b+h+ya/saM myaka saM bud d+ha ya/na maHAr+yA a pa lo ki te shwa ra ya/ha ri ha ri ha ri/
27a7(rgyud): bi d+ha na da ma ka/bo d+hi satwA yA/ma hA sato yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ga ya/Ir+yI mi li/tsi li tsi li/tu le tu le /kun tu le kun tu le/si ri si ri/bi ri bi ri/bi ri re/bi ra ya tsa sha re/
27b1(rgyud): gau ra/gan d+ha ri/pra mi ni ma ti gi/bu ka ka si/kaT Ta ya/ma la caN+Da li/hung tu ma li ni/tsi li mi li/gra na sau ma der sha ne ku ru/tsan+t+ra mu khi la/mu ma Ni ya/mA ti Ar+ya da 
27b2(rgyud): k+Shi ta b+hu d+he/sarba bi d+he naM/ma ma pra sa d+ha ni/sarba bid d+he naM/i shwa ri swAhA/na ma:sarba ta thA ga n+taM/sarba a sha ba ri pu ra ka Na Na/tad+ya thA/o ha ha ha/san ta ya burbe/sat+ya 
27b3(rgyud): a d+hi Sh+Tha na/kri tan ha na sat+ya na/pa tsi pa tsi nan/sarba a sha me pa ri bU ra ya/bud d+ha k+She traM pa ri sho d+ha ya/ma me sa Sh+tsa Ta/pi he Tha na ro hu bud d+ha/a d+hi Sh+Tha na na swAhA/tad+ya thA/
27b4(rgyud): oM dze te/ma hA dze te yan/ma mA ka ya/duSh+tsa ri/taM bA/ba ga du stsa ri staM bA/ma no dus tsha ri staM bA/d+ha rid tra taM b+ha/tad sarbad k+Sha bA ya/a lo ka ya/bi lo ka ya/ta thA 
27b5(rgyud): ga ta d+ha ri sha nan tsa/ma hA ma b+hi kaM/k+Sha mi da bo satwA d+ha ri sha nan/du d+hu ma da d+ha sa me d+ha ri sha me/du d+hu ma da d+has sa me d+ha ri sha ni/sarba ma nu sha la a bi bar du/na ma sarba ta thA ga 
27b6(rgyud): ta naM/na ma:Ar+ya a ba lo ki te shwa ra yA/bo d+hi satwA yA/ma hA satwA ya/ma hA ka ru ni Ni ka ya/sma ra pra ti dz+nyam/ma hA satwA /sid d+han tu me man+t+ra pa de swAhA////sngags 'di 
27b7(rgyud): gur gum tsan dan gyis//gro ga la bris mchod rten ni//sku gdung bcas par bzhug par bya//yang na sku gdung bcas pa yi//spyan ras gzigs dbang sku la gdags//tshes brgyad 
28a1(rgyud): nas ni nya yi bar//bum pa mchod pa rgyas bshams te//dri dang kha dog sna tshogs pa'i//'o thugs zho dang 'bras chan mar//tsan dan dkar po dri yi phur//
28a2(rgyud): gtor ma dkar dang dmar bcas pa'o//gtso bo'i mdun du dpe med ma//g.yas su dung can 'jigs ma g.yon//sna ba'i me tog rgya tsa brgyad//gtor zhing dus gsum mchod te bkru//
28a3(rgyud): de yi rten yang sor drug ste//bdud rnams bzlog par byed pa la//rtse gsum sor brgyad tshang ldan la//thod pa skam po sor drug ste//dbus su rdo rje rtse lnga pa//seng ldeng yu ba khru 
28a4(rgyud): gang ba//de yi rten yang sor drug ste //de yi zur ni brgyad pa la//rdo rje 'khor lo rin po che//pad mar la gri lcags kyu mnga'//mdung rnams g.yas nas bskor bar dgod//
28a5(rgyud): nyi shu tsa gcig brjod nas ni//mtshan mo sngas su bzugs par bya//de nas bcom ldan thub pa ches//dug gi tshogs rnams 'joms pa yi//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs

28a7(rgyud): d+hi satwA yA/ma hA satwA yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/tad+ya thA/oM sarba b+han d+ha nats+tsha da na ka ra yA/sarba pA pa sa mu dra ts+tsha Sha na ka ra yA//sarba byA d+hi pra shA ma na ka ra yA/sarba i t+ya pra shwa 
28b1(rgyud): pa bi na sha ka ra yA/sarba b+ha ye Sh+ya tra na yA/ta s+ya na ma skri twaM/i taM Ar+ya a pa lo ki te shwa ra/ta wa nI la kaN+Na na ma hri dA yAn/a warta ye Sh+ya mi/sarba ar+tha sA d+ha naM/shu b+ha ce ta naM/
28b2(rgyud): sarba b+hu ta naM sarba pA paM marga bi sho d+ha ka/tad+ya thA/a wa lo ki/lo ka ma ti/lo ka ga ti/e h+ya he/ma hA bo d+hi satwa/he bo d+hi satwA+o//he ma hA bo d+hi satwA/he pri ya bo d+hi satwA/he ka ru Ni 
28b3(rgyud): ka sma ra hri da yaM/Ar+ya a wa lo ki ti shwa ra/pa ra ma mA+ai ti tsit+ta ka ru Ni ka/ku ru ku ru karma sA d+ha ya sA d+ha ya/bi d+h+yaM de hi de hi/ma e rad ga ma mang ge ma/bi haM ga ma/sid d+ha yo gi 
28b4(rgyud): shwa ra/du hu du hu/bir yan te/ma hA bing yan te/d+ha ra d+ha ra/d+ha ra ni shwa ra/dz+wa la dz+wa la/bi ma la/a ma la mur te/Ar+ya a ba lo ki te shwa ra/kriSh+na a dzi na/dza Ta ma ku To ta a 
28b5(rgyud): laM kri ta shA ri rA/laM b+ha pra laM b+ha bi laM b+ha/ma hA sid d+ha shud d+ha d+ha ra/ba ha ba la/ma la ma la/ma hA ma la/dz+wa la dz+wa la/ma hA dz+wa la/kriSh+na bar+Na kri Sh+na pA sha nir g+ha tan/he pad ma ha sta/dz+wa ya ka 
28b6(rgyud): ra ni sha tsa re shwa ra/kri Sha sar+pa kri ta yadz+nyo pa bi ta ya/e h+ya hi bA ra ha mu khA/tri pU ra da ha na shwa ra/nA ra ya na/bA la rU pa be shwa d+ha ra/he nI la kaN Sha/he ma hA ha la ha la//bI Sha nir dzi ta lA+o
28b7(rgyud): ka s+ya/rA ga bI Sha nA sha na/dwe Sha bI Sha nA sha na/mo hA bI Sha na sha na/nir mo k+sha na/hu lu hu lu/mu ny+tsa mu ny+tsa/mu hu lu mu hu lu/ha la ha la/ma ha pad ma nA b+ha/sa ra sa ra/si ri si 
29a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ri|su ru su ru/bud d+h+ya bud d+h+ya/bo d+ha bo d+ha/bo d+ha ya bo d+ha ya/bo d+ha ya mi/ta ba nI la kaN+t+wa/e h+ya hi/bA ma s+thi ta siM ha mu khA/ha sa ha sa/muny+tsa muny+tsa/
29a2(rgyud): ma hA/aT+Ta hA sa nir nA di ne/e d+h+ya hi/b+ho b+ho ma hA sid d+ha yo gi shwa ra ya/b+han d+ha b+ha d+ha wa tsaM/sA d+ha ya sA d+ha ya/bi d+yaM sma ra sma ra twaM/he b+ha ga wan/lo ka bi lo ka taM/ta thA ga 
29a3(rgyud): ta/dwa dwa hi me dar sha ni/pra swa d+ha ya me swAhA/sing d+ha ya swAhA/ma hA sid d+ha ya swAhA/sid d+ha yo gi shwa ra ya swAhA/nI la kan ShA swAhA/bA ra ha mu khA ya swAhA/siM ha mu khA ya swAhA/
29a4(rgyud): ma hA nA ra siM hA mu khwa ya swAhA/sing d+ha bid d+ha re swAhA/pad ma hA sta ya swAhA/ma hA pad ma hA sta ya swAhA/badzra hA sta ya swAhA/mahA badzra ha sta ya swAhA/kriSh+na sarba kri ta yadz+nyo pa bi ta yA 
29a5(rgyud): swAhA/ma hA ka la ma ku Ta d+hA ra ya swAhA/tsakra yud d+ha d+hA ra ya swAhA/shaM kha shap+da na ri nA da na kA ra ya swAhA/bo d+ha na kA ra ya swAhA/bA ma skan d+ha de sha s+thI ta kra Sh+Thi adzi na ya swAhA/bA ma ha 
29a6(rgyud): sta byA g+ha tsarma ni bA sa ma ya swAhA/lo kiM shwa ra ya swAhA/ma hA lo kiM ti shwA ra ya swAhA/sarba sadi d+he shwa ra ya swAhA/rak+Sha rak+Sha maM ku ru ku ru rak+Sha murti naM swAhA/na maHAr+ya a wa lo ki te/
29a7(rgyud): shA ra ya/bo d+hi satwA yA/ma hA satwA yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/sid d+h+yan tu men+t+ra pA da ni swAhA////byu ru mu men spur lan dang //shel dang bai dur+ya ka raM//gser gyi snod du chur   
29b1(rgyud): bcas pa//maN+Dal dbus gzhag sngags khri brjod//mug tra rdo yi kong bur ni//chur bcas snga dro gdar byas nas//nyi shu rtsa cig sngags brjod do//khong du 'thungs pas dbyig 
29b2(rgyud): dug dang //sbyar dug lhan cig skyes dug dang //bza' nyes tsam yang sel 'gyur na//dug rigs gzhan lta smos ci dgos//rtson pas the tshom mi bya'o//dad brtson shes rab 
29b3(rgyud): ldan pa'i mis//rtsa ba'i gsang sngags gang la yang //nan tan rtse gcig tu brjod na//de yis thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//gang zhig dad pa'i lcags kyu dang //blo yi re bar   
29b4(rgyud): ldan pa la//lha klu 'bum phrag mthus skyob cing //dkar po dge ba kun slong sems//byang chub rin chen gter thob par//sngags kyi mthus ni mdzad pa ste//sngags la nges dang 
29b5(rgyud): med pa la//bstan na rang nyid nyams par 'gyur//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po//pad ma dra ba zhes bya ba las/rigs kyi khyad par 
29b6(rgyud): gyi sngags 'byung ba'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas bsdus pa'i dngos//'jig rten mgon 
29b7(rgyud): po bcu yi dang //stobs bcu rgyas pa'i rang bzhin can//pha rol phyin bcu lhun grub pa//sa bcu gcig tu rdzogs nas kyang //phyogs bcu kun khyab mdzad pa yi//bcu 
30a1(rgyud): @#/ O /gcig pa yi mtshan nyid nyon//bsod nams skal par ldan pa'i mis//rang gi lus la sngags bgod bya//sngags dang bcas pa'i lus la ni//
30a2(rgyud): sred med bus kyang mi tshugs na//'jig rten gzhan la smos ci dgos//byad dang sha za ro langs dang //'byung pos 'tshe dang grib gnon dang //ltas ngan rnams ni myur du sel//bla 
30a3(rgyud): ma las byung rim pas ni//myur du bla med byang chub 'gyur//bla ma'i lung dang bral bas ni//nams kyang 'grub par mi 'gyur ro//spyi bor yi ge oM dkar po//nan tan du ni bsaM 
30a4(rgyud): par bya/dpral bar yi ge a ljang gu/de bzhin du ni bsam par bya//lce'i steng du yi ge mchog /hri:yig dmar po 'od ldan bsam//snying khar yi ge hUM sngon po//de bzhin 
30a5(rgyud): gsang bar ho ljang gu/gshang ba'i gnas su hUM sngon po//pus po gnyis la swA ser skya//rkang la yi ge ha ljang ser//lag mthil gnyis la hUM dkar po//lag ngar gnyis 
30a6(rgyud): la hri sngon te/dpung par yi ge hri dkod bya//yi ge'i tshogs kyi byin rlabs nas//gang dang gang yang rung ba yi//rtsol ba rnams la 'jug par bya//rtsol ba de dang de dag kyang //
30a7(rgyud): sngags kyi byin brlabs las byung zhing //lus kyang sngags kyi ngo bo nyid//sngags kyi bskyed pa'i lus su byed//sngags dang bcas pa'i lus nyid ni//sngags kyi lus kyis rig 
30b1(rgyud): 'dzin 'gyur//zhing khams du ma sbyangs nas kyang //'gro rnams sngags kyi lus su byed//kun bzang mchog dang mtshungs pa ste//'gro dang dbyer med don rig 'gyur//don de ma lus 
30b2(rgyud): thob pa'i thabs//gang gis rig par 'gyur ba ni//rig 'dzin zhes bya'i dngos po ste//de la gang yang gnas pa'i ming //rig pa 'dzin pa zhes bya ste//sangs rgyas mu stegs pas 
30b3(rgyud): mi shes//lha dang bcas pa'i rtsom pa ni//shin tu ngal ba nyid yin te//ngal ba nyid kyis gol ba'i lam//bgrod par dka' ba de spangs nas//shin tu bag yangs bde ba'i lam//
30b4(rgyud): skal pa dman yang 'grub 'di la//nan tan du ni 'jug par bya//ston pas de skad gsungs pa'i tshe//'khor rnams dga' nas thal sbyar te//mkha' la me tog tor nas kyang //mgon 
30b5(rgyud): po spyan ras gzigs dbang gtso//thams cad gyis ni phyag 'tshal te//bskor ba du ma byas nas kyang //gdong bcu gcig pa zhes bya ba'i//zhal dang sku yi 'du byed ni//mdzes 
30b6(rgyud): bzhin pa la bltas nas kyang //dga' ba'i sems dang ldan pa dag /pus rtsugs thal mo sbyor byas te//phyogs bcur rang rang bde bar 'khod//de nas 'jig rten mgon pos ni//
30b7(rgyud): 'gro ba'i don du rang sngags gsungs//bdag dang gzhan gyi don rdzogs phyir//las kya sbyor ba'ang tha dad do//na mo rang na tra ya yA/na ma Ar+ya dz+nya na sA ga ra tu/'bA+ai ro tsa na/
31a1(rgyud): @#//b+h+ya ha rA dza yA/na ma sarba ta thA g+ha tha/a ra ha te b+h+ya/saM myaka saM bud d+ha ya/na ma a pa lo ki te shwa ra ya/b+ho d+hi swAtwA ya/bam hA ka ru Ni ka yA/tad+ya thA/
31a2(rgyud): oM d+ha ra d+ha ra/d+hi ri d+hi ri/d+hu ru d+hu ru/i T+Ta paT+Te/tsa le tsa le/pra tsa le pra tsa le/ku su me/ku su ma bA re/i li i li/tsi Ti dz+wa la mA pa na ye swAhA/'di bshad tshe na sa chen yang //rnam 
31a3(rgyud): pa drug du g.yos par 'gyur//rtsa ba'i snying po 'di brjod pas//dngos grub ci dang ci 'dod 'grub//gzhan yang sngags rnams kun gyi mgor//sngon gyi rgyal mtshan bzhi po brjod//
31a4(rgyud): na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na ma ar+ya dz+nya na sA ga ra/bA+ai ro tsa na ya/byA ha rA dza ya/ta thA ga ta ya/Ar+ya ha te samyaka saM bud d+ha ya/na ma/Ar+ya a wa lo gi te shwA ra ya/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA satwA 
31a5(rgyud): ya|ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/tad+ya thA/oM ha hA ha hA/i li mi li/tsi li mi li/khi li d+hi li swAhA//dkyil 'khor nang du 'jug pa yis//chu dang gos la bdun bdun brjod//khrus byas 
31a6(rgyud): gos gyon nang du 'jug/snod sogs rdzas gzhan dag kyang bsang //na mo rat+na tra ya yA//na ma:Ar+ya dz+nya na sA ga ra/bai ro tsa na ya/byA ha ra dza ya/ta thA ga ta yA/ar ha te saM myaka 
31a7(rgyud): saM bud d+ha yA/na ma Ar+ya a wa lo ki te shwA ra ya/bo d+hiM satwA yA/ma hA satwA yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/tad+ya thA/oM d+hu ru d+hu ru/ha hA ha hA swAhA/bdun phrag re re btab pa'i sa/spos sreg 
31b1(rgyud): pa dang mar me spar//na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na ma Ar+ya dz+nya na sa ga ra bA+ai ro tsa na yA/b+h+yu ha ra tsa ya/na ma sarba d+ha thA ga ha a ra ha te b+h+ya/saM myaka saM bud d+ha ya/na ma Ar+ya a ba lo ki te shwa 
31b2(rgyud): ra ya/bo d+hi satwA+o ya/ma hA swAtwA ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/tad+ya thA/oM si ri si ri/d+hi ri d+hi ri/si d+hi ri si d+hi ri/d+ha ra swAhA/me tog byug spos me tog phreng //gzhan yang mchod pa'i rdzas 
31b3(rgyud): rnams la//bdun bdun brjod nas sku gzugs la//gtor dang byug dang gdags par bya//na mo rat+na tra ya ya/na maHAr+ya dz+nya na sA ga ra//bA+ai ro tsa na ya byu hA rA dza ya/ta thA ga ta 
31b4(rgyud): ya/a ra ha ta te b+h+ya/sa myaka saM bung d+ha yA/na ma Ar+ya a wa lo ki te shwa ra yA/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA satwA ka ru Ni ka ya/ta d+ya thA/oM sa d+he sa d+he/si d+hi si d+hi/su d+hu su d+hu swAhA/
31b5(rgyud): bshos gtsang me tog 'bras bur bcas//nyi shu gcig brjod zhal zas mchod//na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na ma Ar+ya a wa la ko ti shwa ra yA/byu ha rA dza yA/ta thA ga ta ya/ar 
31b6(rgyud): had te b+h+ya/saM myaka saM bud d+ha yA/na maHAr+ya dz+nya na lo ki tA+e shwa ra yA/bo d+hi sa twA yA/ma hA satwA yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/tad+ya thA/oM ma si ta si/tsa le tsa le/hu ru hu ru/tsu ru 
31b7(rgyud): tsu ru/su ru su ru/mu ru mu ru swAhA/me tog dkar po'i shing dum bu/sum cu zho mar sbrang rtsir ni/zhag cig spangs nas nyin cig gam//zhag re dum bu re re//sngags re btab nas bsreg+sa 
32a1(rgyud): @#/ O /na bzod//ci dgos las rnams bsgrubs na 'grub//na mo rat+na tra ya yA//na maHAr+ya dz+nyA na sA ga ra/bA+ai ro tsa na byu ha rA dza yA/ta thA ga ta yA/ar 
32a2(rgyud): ha te b+h+ya/saM myaka saM bud d+ha yA/na ma Ar+ya a ba lo ki te shwa ra ya/be b+h+yi satwA yA//ma hA satwA yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA/tad+ya thA/oM i li mi li/bi li ti li/tsi li hi li swAhA//chu 
32a3(rgyud): 'am thal ba yungs kar la//lan bdun bsngags nas phyogs bzhir gtor//sa phyogs nye ring gang du yang //'tshams gcod pa yi mchog yin no//na mo rat+na tra ya ya/bA+ai 
32a4(rgyud): ro tsa na byu hA ra dza ya/na maHsarba ta thA ga ta ya/a ra ha te b+h+ya/saM myaka saM pung d+ha ya/na maHAr+ya a wa lo ki te shwa ra yA/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA satwA yA/ma ha ka ru Ni ka yA/ta d+ya thA/
32a5(rgyud): oM bi ti si ti/ti Te ti Te/tsi Te tsi Te/be te be te/ka tsa ka tsa/b+ha ga war Ar+ya a ba le ki te shwa ra sarba swa b+ha naM swAhA//sku sogs slar yang spyan drang dang //rang nyid gnas nas 'dod don phyir//
32a6(rgyud): chu la lan bdun btab pa yis//bkrus nas dgongs pa de dag bya//na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na ma Ar+ya dz+nyA na sA ga ra/bA+ai ro tsa na yA/byu hA ra dza yA/ta thA ga ta yA/a ra ha te b+h+ya/saM myika 
32a7(rgyud): saM bud d+hu ya/na maHAr+ya a lnga lo ki te shwa ra yA/be d+hi satwA ya/ma ha satwA ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/tad+ya thA/oM dza hA hri ho//'jig rten dbang la rab dga' bas/zas skom byin   
32b1(rgyud): gyi brlabs pa'i sngags//de nas bcom ldan thub pa ches//sdig pa ma lus bcom pa yi/ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs nas//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje las//mgon po zhal bcu 
32b2(rgyud): gcig pa yi//rtsa ba'i rig sngags 'di gsungs so//na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na ma:Ar+ya dz+nyA na sA ga ra/bA+ai ro tsa na byu ha rA dzA ya/ta thA ga ta yA/a ra ha de b+h+ya/saM myaka saM bud d+ha ya/na ma:
32b3(rgyud): A r+ya a wa lo ki te shwA ra ya/bo d+hi swAtwA yA/ma hA swAtwA ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/tad+ya thA/na ma sarba bud d+ha bo d+hi satwo/a haM bud d+ha/shra ba ka saM g+he b+h+yo//na ma Ar+ya dz+nyA nas ga ra/
32b4(rgyud): bA+ai ro tsa na byu ha ra dza ya/ta thA ga ta ya/a ra ha de b+h+ya/saMmyaka saM bud d+ha yA/na ma Ar+ya a wa lo ki te shwAr yA/bo d+hi satwA yA/ma hA satwA yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ga yA/a b+ha yA d+ha na/
32b5(rgyud): sarba b+ha ta ba ra tra naM ka raM/sarba Ar+ya ya b+ha ban tsa pra sha ma Na ka raM/sarba pi rud thA ka la ha bA+e ba d+ha/bi ga grA ha pa shA ma na ka raM/sarba pA pa bi nA sha ya ka raM/sarba b+hU ta bai ta la bi shA ma 
32b6(rgyud): Na/dur swa pa na/dur Ni mi TaM/sarba bi g+ha nan bI na yA kan/bo hon sa ka raM pa ra tsa kraM/dur b+he k+Sha sho ka bA ya ni ka naM/sarba de ba/na ga ya k+Sha/rak+Sha sa/gan d+ha ba/a sU ru/ga ru Da/kin 
32b7(rgyud): nA ra/ma hA+o ra ga sarba ts+tshen d+ha na kA raM/sarba bI khra ke Te b+ha sug k+Sha g+har d+ha/kre ts+tsha ka lir bA+ai Ta li/a mU ka li bi nA sha ka ra yA/na mo nA thA ka ru Na/A+e ka dwa sha mu kha/sa hA sra b+hu dze/sa 
33a1(rgyud): @#/ O /hA sra nai traM/tad+ya thA/oM dzA ya kA ri/yud d+had tA ri ni/bi tsid tra mu khe/e ka dwa sha mu kha/sarba maM ga la d+hA ri ni/dzI mud te/sa hA sra b+hu dze/sa hA sra nai traM/sa hA sra shI 
33a2(rgyud): nan laM kA ra/bi b+hu ShI te/ma Ni kuN+Da li/san nA ta sa nad te/bi tsi t+t+ra mA+au la d+ha re/tsan+t+ra sUr+ya a ti re ke/a kA sha ga gA na tsA ri ni/maM ga la d+ha ri dzi mu te/pa bid tra kri ta maM ga le/b+hu ri mi tra ka 
33a3(rgyud): r+She/tsu ru tsu ru shwe ta pa Te/mu ru mu ru shwe d+ha a ba nad d+he/hu ru hu ru pa bing tra mu khe/ku ru ku ru tsaN+Da mu khi/A di t+ya pA sha bar ti ni/dzaM b+ha ni maM ga la d+ha ri ni/staM b+ha ni sarba bi d+ya d+harma te/mo ha ni a mo 
33a4(rgyud): g+ha pa sha ha ste/sarba b+ha ya bi mo tsa nI/rA dza tsA+au ra ag+ni u da ka b+ha ya pra shA ma ni/ka khorda ni bA ra NI/oM hi ri hi ri dza ka ri/bA la la ka ri/d+ha na ka ri/dza sa ka ri/b+ha ba da pra sha ma NI/yud d+ha ni wA 
33a5(rgyud): ra nI/pa ra tsA kra pra shA ma nI/mA ra s+yai na bi nA sha nI//yud d+ho da tA ra ni/bra b+hU ta be lA/ma Ni ma Ni/ma hA ma Ni/ma Ni d+ha ri/tri lo ka a lo ka kA ri/tri pu ra nA ka ra/bi d+haM sa ni/pa ra s+ya na bi drA 
33a6(rgyud): pa ni/sarba krit+ya karma ni sU da ni/sarba byA d+hI naM pra sha swAhA/oM tsi li mi li swAhA/tsi li tsi li swAhA/mi li mi li swAhA/ci li tsi li swAhA/tsi khi li tsi mi li swAhA/tsaN+Da li ma taM gi swAhA/u  
33a7(rgyud): dA ke shi paM bi li/pi ga lA kA+e te d+hi mu khi swAhA/oM ka ra ka ra/tsa ra tsa ra/sa ra sa ra/si ri si ri/su ru su ru/muny+tsa muny+tsa/muny+tsa pa ya/muny+tsa pa ya/pra sha ma ya/pra shA ma ya/yud d+hod 
33b1(rgyud): tA ra ni phaT/tsin d+ha nir ma la ka rI phaT/a su ra nir g+ha tA ni phaT/dza ya ka ri phaT/skri t+ya ka rI phaT/sa hA sra shi re phaT/sa hA sra b+hu dze phaT/sa ha sra nai tra phaT/sarba bad+yi d+ha ra nam skri te phaT/
33b2(rgyud): sarba b+ha ya bi mo k+Sha ni phaT/pa ra tsA kra ni bA ra ni phaT/pa rA s+yA na bi dra pa ni phaT/sarba kri t+ya karma sU da nI phaT/sarba byA d+hi pra shA ma nI phaT/sarba byA b+hi bi nA sha ya phaT/sarba byA d+hi pra ma tha ni   
33b3(rgyud): phaT/sarba bra hod ts+ye da ni phaT/rak+Sha rak+Sha maM/sarba bud d+ha bo d+hi satwA nan/sa mA tA pi tri naM/so pa s+tha yi kA nA/sa pa ri wA rA lAM/te dze sa ma ma sha rI yaM/sarba b+ha yo//u pa dra be b+h+ya/sarba   
33b4(rgyud): yud d+he b+h+ya/sarba gra he b+h+ya/sarba byA d+hi b+h+ya/sarba du skri te b+h+yaHsarba kri t+ya kA khor te b+h+ya/sarba bI khe b+h+ya/sarba ka wa ka tsit+ta pI De b+h+ya/sh+tsa rak+Sha rak+Sha maM swAhA/sa ha sa b+hu dze/sa ha sra nA+e tra/A+e ka 
33b5(rgyud): dwa sha ma khi/ar+ya a wa lo ki te shwa ra ya/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA satwA ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/sid d+han tu me mAn tra/pa da ni swAhA////rje btsun zhal bcu gcig pa'i rtsa ba'i sngags  
33b6(rgyud): 'di yon tan ni mtha' yas te/gang la 'di ni gtsug tor du//yid bzhin nor bu thogs dang mtshungs//'chang dang klog par byed pa la//mtha' yas yon tan rnyed par 'gyur//nad   
33b7(rgyud): dang gdon rnams sel ba dang //khyad par du ni sdig kun byung //ba dkar mo yi bdud rtsi lngas//maN+Dal 'jam po lnga byugs las//gtsang sbra ldan pas dbus su ni//shing sku ring bsrel 
34a1(rgyud): ldan pa yi//sku gdung bzhi ni phyogs bzhir bzhag/tshad dang ldan pa'i bum pa ni//lnga pa dbus dang 'tshams su ste//rigs lnga'i mdog dang phyag rgya 
34a2(rgyud): can//sna tshogs rgyan dang mchod pa dang //srad bu tha dad bri bar bya//ci 'dod phyag la 'brel byas te//tshes cig nas ni bcu bzhi'i bar//'byod pas dmigs nas sngags brjod de//
34a3(rgyud): bzhi khri rdzogs nas bco lnga la//'dod pas gsol btab khrus byas na//ci 'dod myur du 'grub pa dang //bdud dang bar du gcod pa dang //rnam par 'tsho dang mi mthun pa//khrus kyis

34a5(rgyud): yang //gshis ngan 'chi ba bco lnga med//skye bzangs bco lnga thob par 'gyur//mu ge rgyal po'i chad pa dang //dug dang mtshon dang g.yul 'gyed pa//stag dang sbrul sogs gcan 
34a6(rgyud): zan gdug/dug can zas dang me chus 'chi//srog chags gdug dang nyal nyes dang //g.yang sa byad dang lha 'dres gtses//sor mi rung dang bdag lce ba rnams//de la nam yang mi 
34a7(rgyud): 'byung ngo //rgyal po chos ldan yul skye zhing //'phags pa bzhugs dang dus bzangs yul//bshes gnyen bzang dang dbang po tshang //dge la dga' zhing tshul khrim ldan//bran khol 
34b1(rgyud): mang zhing nor mang rtag/gzhan gyis bkur zhing dgos pa sbyor//sems bde 'jig rten skyong bas bsrung /sangs rgyas zhal mthong don zab rtogs//gzhan la sman la byams sems che//
34b2(rgyud): snying rje'i mchi ma 'khrug pa dang //yon tan dran nas spro ba dang //dkon mchog gsum la dam pa dang //phul du byung ba'i blo 'phel dang //tshul bzhin ma yin yid bral dang //bka' lung

34b4(rgyud): 'dzin pa drag po'i blo byung tshe//sngags 'dis stong pa nyid du bya//oM shu n+ya dz+nya na badzra swa b+ha wa ad ma kA+o hA//gang zhig lus ngag yid g.yos na//sngags 'dis brtan par byin gyis brlab//

35a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa'i zur dang phyogs bzhi por//g.yas su phyogs par gzugs par bya////na mo rat+na tra ya yA/na maHAr+ya dz+nya na sA gA ra/bai ro tsa na ya byu ha rA dzA ya/na maHsarba

35a3(rgyud): ha na ha na/da ha da ha/pa tsa pa tsa/a mri te hUM phaT swA hA//ltas ngan thams cad 'joms pa dang //gnas khang byin gyis brlabs nas su/tshe dang longs spyod brtan pa dang //dpal
