35b7(rgyud): la ni rnam par 'jug/yul drug dag gi dbang byas nas//nyon mongs rnam pa drug dang bcas/nyon mongs rgyas par gyur ba las//las gsum mngon par 'dus byas pas//phung 
36a1(rgyud): @#/ O /po rnam pa drug dang ldan//phung po'i grangs kyi dbye bas ni//yan lag drug drug 'phel bar 'gyur//yan lag rdzogs pa'i grib mas ni//rang bzhin 
36a2(rgyud): dag pa'i sems sgrib byed//sgrib gnyis go cha 'thub po ni//bsal dkra'i ri bo dang bcas pa//rtag chad gnas su zhugs pa'o//srog rtsol bsdams pa'i sbyor ba yis//phyi rol 
36a3(rgyud): snang ba nub par 'gyur//rtsol de bral nas dga' skyes te//yi ge la ni sems gnas 'gyur//yi ge gsal ba'i 'od zer ni//ba spu'i khung rnams thams cad nas//sems dang bcas 
36a4(rgyud): pa ldang 'jug te//slar yang thig ler bsdus nas ni//yi ge nyid kyang mi dmigs so//'dzin pa de las thob nas kyang //sgyu ma'i rnam par rjes dran bya//de la goms nas ting nge 'dzin ni//
36a5(rgyud): nyams pa med pa thob par 'gyur//ting nge 'dzin de brtan gyur nas ni//lus 'dis rig pa 'dzin pa thob//rig 'dzin de la gnas nas ni//bla med byang chub des thob 'gyur//skad cig  
36a6(rgyud): de la dngos rnams ni//namakha' lta bur dag nas kyang //mkha' ltar kun la khyab par spyod//rten ni spyan ras gzigs dbang ste//lus ni 'phags pa 'jam dpal lo//yan lag phyag 
36a7(rgyud): na rdo rje ste//mgo dang thod ni ma pham pa//btsug tor sgrib pa rnam sel nyid//nyi zla kun tu bzang po ste//sa chu me rlung namakha'i dang //rnam shes nyid dang bcas pa'o//thams cad dbyer med 
36b1(rgyud): gyur pa yi//bsam gtan la ni gnas pa'i phyir//rtsol rnams so sor bsdu ba'i phyir//sems rlod pas ni nges par 'grub//'das dang ma 'ongs da ltar gyi//dus ni rtag pa ma yin no//mi 
36b2(rgyud): rtog sems gnyis 'brel gyur na//dus la tha dad mi rtog go/nyi ma'i 'khor lo chu rgyun dang //nam mkha'i phyogs ni ji lta bar//sems kyang sems kyis btsal bar bya//gzhan gyis 
36b3(rgyud): go skabs mi rnyed do//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po pad ma dra ba zhes bya ba las//thun mong sngags 'byung ba'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////
36b4(rgyud): //de nas drang srong nang nas ni//sna tshogs gzugs ldan zhes bya ba//bskal pa du mar sbyangs pa yi//byang chub sems dpa' rig 'dzin zhig/rgya che rang gi 
36b5(rgyud): 'khor bcas pa//dkyil 'khor drung du lhags nas ni//rmad byung mchod pa'i tshogs kyis mchod//phyag 'tshal bskor ba du ma byas//thal sbyar 'dzum pa'i mdangs kyis ni//ston pa 
36b6(rgyud): la ni 'di skad gsol//oM mtha' yas spros pa nyer zhi zhing //zhi zhing dul mdzad rab spyod mdzad//rtog spyod rtog mkhyen rtog spong bzhugs//ye shes kun rgyas phyag 'tshal lo//zhi 
36b7(rgyud): la zhi 'gyur mtha' 'byung ba//chos rnams kun mchog zhi chos che//spros med las spros dkar bcu khyab//mtha' yas chos la phyag 'tshal lo//grol 'gyur grol mdzad dag pa che//dag par 
37a1(rgyud): spyod pa gcad mgon tshogs rdzogs tshogs ldan tshogs spyod rgyal//tshogs rtag bdag nyid phyag 'tshal lo//rab mdzes thugs rje'i nyi mas brgyan//'jig 
37a2(rgyud): rten rab gsal mdzad pa po//rtag pa don chen rtag bzhugs pa//mgon po spyan ras gzigs phyag 'tshal//khams gsum bag chags kyis dkrugs la//thibs po'i ling tog zhugs 
37a3(rgyud): pa rnams//mngon du snang bar brjod pa dang //sems la gsal 'gyur shin tu dka'//mngon du med la brjod 'brel yang //lkog tu gyur pa smos ci bgos//lkog don du byed pa la//
37a4(rgyud): phyag rgya dang ni 'dra 'bag mchog/mngon gsum thob ldan myong nas kyang //don la sems kyi gsol ba skye//don ni gang yang goms pa las//sems kyi 'jug pa brtag pa ste//bde gsal 
37a5(rgyud): mi rtog blo 'bras bu//bsgom pa'i pha rol phyin pa'o//blo dman rnams ni bzhugs pa dang //'bring po grol bar bya phyir dang //rnon po rdzogs sangs rgyas 'gyur phyir//gzugs brnyan 
37a6(rgyud): 'byung tshul bshad du gsol//myur du 'jug dang grol ba dang //myur du byang chub cher 'gyur ba//gzugs brnyan dges pa'i tshul 'byung can//'dren pas de ring bshad du gsol//mkha' 
37a7(rgyud): sprin rtag pa med pa la//mtshan mar bzung du med pa ltar//rnam rtog gis dkrug sems la ni//su zhig mtshan mar 'dzin par nus//dmigs pa'i mtshan ma gsal byed pa//gzugs brnyan 
37b1(rgyud): tshad dang ldan pa ste//legs par gyur pa'i gzugs la ni/yid 'jug byin rlabs nges par 'jug/de nas bcom ldan 'dren pa gtso//ljags kyi dbang po rab bkram nas//gzugs brnyan   
37b2(rgyud): dge ba'i tshul 'byung ba//nga las nyan par brtson pa ni//legs so lha yi drang sreng mchog/rig 'dzin byang chub sems khyod legs//rtse cig sems kyis mnyan nas kyang //legs par zung 
37b3(rgyud): la yid la zhog/khyod la yang dag bshad bya ba//byin brlabs dges pa'i sku gzugs ni//tshon gyis ris su bris pa 'am//rin chen ris sam rdo ba 'am//shing las byas pa mchog tu   
37b4(rgyud): sngags//dang po'i las can sems can la//dmigs pa gsal bar 'gyur ba dang //byin brlabs mchog ni 'jug par 'gyur//tsan dan dkar po gsal zhing drang //gzhon zhing chag 
37b5(rgyud): gas med pa la//bsangs shing dud bas bdugs nas ni//dad ldan dang tshul bzang pos gzhog/'jig rten dbang phyug mgon gyi sku//bco brgyad sor tshad ldan pa la//gdan khri dag ni 
37b6(rgyud): sor dgu ste//rgyab yol sor tshad drug gis bskyed//bzhengs te ldem bag nyams dga' ba//rin chen du sku gzugs brgyan//g.yas kyi dang po bgrang phreng ste//gnyis pa mi 'jigs 
37b7(rgyud): rtsol ba'i phyag/g.yon gyi dang po pad ma dmar//gnyis pa ril ba spyi blugs thogs//zhal gyi grangs ni bcu gcig la//bdun gyi zhal gsum mtshan bzangs can//byams pa   
38a1(rgyud): snying rje dga' ba mdangs//dkar po dkar ser dmar skya'o//g.yon gyi zhal gsum khro bo'i zhal//sgug pa'i mdangs dang ldan pa ste//dmar dang dmar ser le ba rgan 
38a2(rgyud): no//g.yas su gtum pa'i khro zhal gsum//sngo dang ser dang ljang gu'o//rgyab tu khro gtum mdog dmar ba//rta skad rang tu 'tsher bar zhal//khro bo'i zhal rnams mche ba gtsigs//mig 
38a3(rgyud): gsum dbu skra ser po ni//gyen du thor cog bcings pa'o//steng du sangs rgyas 'dzum pa'i zhal//byi ru bcag pa'i 'tshams 'dra'o//dbu yi cod pan nang rnams su//'di dag 
38a4(rgyud): phyogs bcu'i thub pa rnams//re re bzhin du bzhugs par bya//dbu rgyan cod pan la sogs dang //rna rgyan mgul rgyan dpung rgyan dang //lag rgyan se mo dor bcas pa//do shal rkang gdub ske 
38a5(rgyud): rags so//rgyan dang na bza' mdzes pas brgyan//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//ris ma dag la dga' gyur na//yid gzhungs dal spyod khro ba med//sred chung shes rab snying rje che//
38a6(rgyud): thos ldan ri mo'i rgyud la mkhas//bsam pa bzang ldan tshul khrims srung //'bri chos gsob la gzhungs pa yi//dben zhing nyams dga'i gnas su bri//gzugs bzang yid dal gtsang 
38a7(rgyud): ldan pa'i//bu mos bkal ba'i ras la ni//tshon bzangs dur smrig kha dog bya//'thas par byas te 'dzam por bya//thig gis phyogs cha bcad nas kyang //ser skya'i tshon gyis gzug 
38a8(rgyud): brnyen ni//tshad la mkhas pas tshad ldan par//nan tan du ni 'dri bar bya//kha dog dkar po shel yin te//dmar ser rtsi ni li khri'o//sngon po rams dang 'thing shing ste//ser po bab bla dge 
38b1(rgyud): pa'o//nag po an+dza nyid yin te//ljang gu rams dang ldong ros so//yang na spangs ma nyid kyis bya//smug rtsi rams dang rgya rkyags sbyar//gzhan yang ci rigs sbyar bar bya//sbyin dang bral
38b2(rgyud): na grugs par 'gyur//shing sbyin ser dang ka pid tha//lo mi tam dang pAn dza ra//de dag sa sar sbyar ba yis//gsal zhing grugs med dag par byed//a ta sas ni smug par byed//na to nas
38b3(rgyud): ni dag par 'gyur//rgyas par byed na gru bzhi pa//yang na sgo gcig pa yang rung //dkyil 'khor ta ris gsum pa 'am//yang na gnyis sam gsum yang rung //dbus su gdod bcu gcig 
38b4(rgyud): pa ni//mdzes pa'i lha mo bzhi ldan bri//shar du spyan ras gzigs dbang gi/dkyil 'khor rnam snang gtso bo de yang spyan sogs lha mo bzhi'i//phyag rgya bzhi dang ldan pa'o//lhor ni 'jig rten
38b5(rgyud): dbang phyug gi/dkyil 'khor shAg thub gtso bo can/lcags kyu zhags pa lcags sgrogs dang //'bebs pa'i phyag rgyar ldan pa'o//nub du rig pa'i dkyil 'khor ni//sems pa bzhi bzhir
38b6(rgyud): bcas pa yi//dbus su rdo rje dbang mo yang //mdun du rdo rje rtag pa ste//g.yas su rdo rje dpal 'byung ngo //rgyab du rdo rje dpag med te//g.yon du rdo rje don grub po//shar du lha mo spyan ma ni//
38b7(rgyud): mdun du rdo rje sems dpa'o//g.yas su rdo rje rgyal po'o//rgyab tu rdo rje chags pa ste//g.yon du rdo rje legs pa'o//lhor ni lha mo ma ma ki/bdun du rdo rje rin chen no//g.yas su rdo rje nyi 
38b8(rgyud): ma ste//rgyab du rdo rje rgyal mtshan no//g.yon du rdo rje bzhan pa'o//nub du na bza' dkar mo yi//mdun du rdo rje chos yin te//g.yas su rdo rje rnon po yin//rgyab tu rdo rje sgyu zhes pa//g.yon du rdo
39a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rje smra ba'o//byang du lha mo sgrol ma yi//mdun du rdo rje las yin te//g.yas su rdo rje bsrung ba'o//rgyab du rdo rje gnon sbyin no//g.yon du rdo rje khu tshur ro//
39a2(rgyud): 'khor gyi byang chub sems dpa' rnams//kun kyang zhal cig phyag bzhi ste//g.yas kyi dang po rang rigs rtags//gnyis pa thams cad skyabs sbyin te//g.yon gyi dang po pad ma 
39a3(rgyud): yang //phyogs kyi kha dog ldan pa'o//gnyis pas phyogs kyi mdog ldan pa'i//sngags bshor phreng ba 'chang ba'o//'khor gyi lha mo de dag kun//zhal gcig pa la phyag bzhi 
39a4(rgyud): ma|g.yon pa'i tha mas bum pa 'chang //lhag ma gong ltar shes par bya//byang du khro bo'i dkyil 'khor yang /khro mo bzhi bzhir bcas pa'o//dbus su rdo rje hUM mdzad te//sngon po 
39a5(rgyud): ud pa la yi mdog/mdun du gsang sngags chen mo ste//g.yas su rdo rje khab mo'o//rgyab tu rdo rje brjed byed mo//g.yon du rdo rje mthu chen mo//shar gyi 'khor lo'i cha rtsibs la//
39a6(rgyud): khro bo gshin rje gshed po nag/mdun du rdo rje dbang zhal ma//g.yas su rdo rje khyab 'jug mo//brgyab tu rdo rje gzhon nu mo//g.yon du rdo rje bskul byed ma//'khor lo rtsibs ('chang)gnyis pa 
39a7(rgyud): la//khro bo gzhan gyis mi thub ser//mdun du rdo rje dgag byed ma//g.yas su rdo rje me chen mo//rgyab du rdo rje zla ba mo//g.yon du rdo rje gshin rje ma//rtsibs gsum rdo rje gtsug tor ser//
39b1(rgyud): rdo rje 'khor mo khrag 'thung mo//rdo rje rlung 'dzin chen mo dang //rdo rje snang byed mo bcas pa'o//nub kyi rtsibs la rta mgrin dmar//mdun du rdo rje rmongs byed ma//g.yas su rdo rje zhi ba mo//
39b2(rgyud): rgyab tu rdo rje mi pham ma//g.yon du rdo rje tshol byed ma//lnga pa'i rtsibs la de bzhin du//khro bo bgegs mthar byed pa ljang //mdun du rdo rje gsor byed ma//g.yas su rdo rje'i gtum mo ste//
39b3(rgyud): rgyab tu rdo rje ro langs ma//g.yon du rdo rje rengs byed ma//rtsibs drug 'khor lor khro bo ni//rdo rje gnod mdzes rgyal ljang sngo//mdun du rdo rje chu dbang mo//g.yas su rdo rje grub 'byung ma//
39b4(rgyud): rgyab tu rdo rje mtshan mo dga'//g.yon du rdo rje mkha' dbang phyug/de ltar khro bo khro mo rnams//kun kyang zhal cig phyag bzhi ste//khro bo phyag gnyis mda' gzhu bkang //g.yas 
39b5(rgyud): na be con kha T+waM dang //tho ba rdo rje gya gram dang //'khor gtun shing rdo rje brnams//rang rtags phyag rgya 'chang ba'o//g.yon na sdigs 'dzub zhags pa'o//kun kyang stag gi sham 
39b6(rgyud): thabs can//khro mo rnams kyi phyag rgya ni//g.yas na gri gug cang te'u//g.yon na thong ba kha T+waM ngo //zhing lpags dag gi chos gos so//'jig gi lpags pa'i sham thabs can//
39b7(rgyud): khro bo'i g.yon na sgeg pa'i tshul//de rgyab cha yi snam bu la//phyogs rer lha mo bzhi dang //de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' bzhi//phyogs rer brgyad brgyad bkod pa ste//sum 
40a1(rgyud): @#//cu rtsa gnyis bzhengs pa'i tshul//de bzhin sgo bzhir sems ma mchog/lcags kyu zhags pa lcags sgrog dang //'bebs ma rang rtags phyag rgya g.yas//
40a2(rgyud): gnyis pa mi 'jig phyag rgya ste//g.yon pa phyogs mdog pad ma dang //gnyis pas dar gyi chos gos so//kun kyang sgo mnyams ldan par bri//de rgyab dkyil 'khor cha la ni//shar 
40a3(rgyud): du rgya byin glang po zhon//g.yas kyi dang po rdo rje ste//gnyis pa skyabs sbyin phyag rgya can//g.yon pa phyogs mdog bad ma ste//gnyis pa rang mdog phreng ba bri//de yi g.yon du tshangs 
40a4(rgyud): pa ser//ngang pa'i khri la bzhugs pa ste//g.yas kyi dang po nyi ma 'atang //me'i phyogs su me lha ste//mdog dmar ra zhon me phung 'dzin//lho yi phyogs su gshin rje sngon//thod dbyug 
40a5(rgyud): bzung ba ma he zhon//bden bral du ni srin po nag/rtse gsum bzung nas ro la zhon//nub du chu lha dkar po ni//klu yi khri la sbrul nag bzung //de yi g.yon du sa bdab skar//bum pa 
40a6(rgyud): bzung ste rus sbal zhon//lha min phyogs su rlung lha ljang //ba dan bzung ste sha ba zhon//byang du gnod sbyin mi la zhon//ljang nag phyag na gter sgom mo//byang shar dbang ldan dkar 
40a7(rgyud): po ni//rtse gsum bzung ste khyu mchog zhon//sgo bzhir rgyal po chen po bzhi//phyogs kyi kha dog go cha can//pi wang ral gri zhags pa dang //rin chen dbyug to g.yas kyis 'chang //
40b1(rgyud): gnyis pas skyabs kyi phyag rgya ste//g.yon pa pad ma bgrang phreng can//phyogs kyi kha dog ldan pa'o//kun kyang sgeg nyams phyogs kyi mdog/rim bzhin stag gzig don seng ge'i//
40b2(rgyud): khri la bzhugs pa'i gzigs pa can//rang rang phyogs kyi sgo la ni//bsrung brtson gshin tu gus pa'o//dkyil 'khor cha brgyad khor yug tu//bza' shing me tog spos shing dang //
40b3(rgyud): mdzes pa'i shing tshogs tshal gseb du//mdzes pa'i bya sogs dud 'gre dang //lha yi bu dang bu mo'i tshogs//de bzhin klu dang gnod sbyin dang //dri za grul bum bu bu mo//rol mo byed

40b5(rgyud): snar ma nor bu mgo ud pa la//nam so pad dkar lag tri shul//rgyal ni bgrang phreng skag sgul nag/mchu ni pad ser gri mnyam bzhag/me bzhi pad dkar dbo' dbyug pa//nag

40b7(rgyud): mon gre shel sgong khrums stod kyi//ral gri khrums smad kum mu ta//nam gru ba dan tha skar gyi//shing lo bra nye'i lcug phran no//zla ba ku mud dkar po ste//nyi ma pad ma dmar po'o//
41a1(rgyud): @#//mig dmar me'i phung po ste//gza' lag ud dpal sngon po'o//phur bu chos kyi glegs bam ste//pa wa sangs ni bgrang phreng 'dzin//spen pa lag na ral gri 
41a2(rgyud): 'chang //sgrags can bdud rtsi'i gzhong bu'o//nang gi grA yi char gtogs pa//bkra shis lha mo brgyad yin te//rtag dga' ma dang rgyal ma shar//don grub ma dang mi pham lhor//
41a3(rgyud): nub du bde ma bde dga' ma//myur 'grub ma dang brtan ma byang //rtse lnga rtse gsum rdo rje dang //sngon po ser po rin chen dang //dkar dang dmar po ud pal dang //ud pal sngo dang ku 
41a4(rgyud): mu tA//dang po'i phyag na rim bzhin 'chang //skyabs sbyin pad ma bgrang phreng ni//lhag ma rnams kyis rim bzhin 'chang //de dag thams cad phyogs mdog go/rgyas pa'i spros pas 
41a5(rgyud): skyo na yang //lnga bcu brgyad dam sum cu bdun//nyi shu dgu 'am nyi shu lnga//bco lnga dgu 'am lnga gcig te//yi ge phyag rgya gzugs brnyan sogs//skul pa sku yi cho 'phrul te//
41a6(rgyud): rang gis ji ltar 'dod pa yi//sems kyis byin pa nyid du 'gyur//bder gshegs dang po ting 'dzin dang //phyag rgya rtsigs pa rab gnas bya//nyams sam dri mas gos pa ni//bkru 
41a7(rgyud): bas bzhig nas dag byed la//rtog pa med pa'i sems kyis su//de la bsod nams dpag tu med//gzugs sogs sngon pas dman gyur yang //de yis bsod nams mtha' 
41b1(rgyud): yas thob//phrin las dang ni sku gsang ba//de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan nyid de//de bzhin nyid de de stong nyid//byang chub nams mkha'i mtshan nyid do//mtshan dang dpe byad kun rdzogs 
41b2(rgyud): te||sgyu ma rmi lam lta bu'o//sum cu gnyis dang brgyad bsdu'o//yi ge'i brda' dang sgros pa'i sgra//longs sku bsod nams rgyu mthun 'bras//sprul sku las kyi rnam smin 'bras//
41b3(rgyud): kun rdzob don dam mtshan nyid ldan//dag dang ma dag skal ldan la//bder gshegs phrin las gzugs sku gnyis//tshogs gnyis las ni sku gsum byung //rnam byung kun nyon 
41b4(rgyud): mongs pas stong //lus ngag yid kyi spyod yul min//dmigs med nam mkha'i khams rtogs pa//rgyal ba rnams kyi chos sku'o//rtag chad med pa'i rten 'brel ni//sgye 'gag

41b6(rgyud): kun slong byang chub sems kyi rin chen nor//thob par 'dis mdzad 'khor los bsgyur ba 'byung //sems kyi dbus su gces phyir dal spyod pa//dam par bya ba'i kun khyad par du//pad 
41b7(rgyud): ma'i dra ba phyag stong gis brgyan pa//myur du 'grub pa'i 'jig rten skyong ba la sogs//thams cad dga' bar mdzad pa smos ci dgos//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi rtsa 
42a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po pad ma dra ba zhes bya ba las//gzugs brnyan 'byung ba'i le'u ste lnga pa'o////de nas dga' ldan gnas 
42a2(rgyud): 'khrungs pa'i//bskal pa du mar tshogs bsags pa//rig pa gsum ldan lha yi bu//mdzes pa'i togs ces mtshan nyid pa//spyan ras gzigs la cher dang nas//bzhengs te   
42a3(rgyud): thal mo sbyar byas nas//'khor tshogs chen po dang bcas pa//mgrin gcig tu ni 'di skad gsol//oM khams gsum kun gyi bla ma tso//'dren pa mgon po rdzogs sangs rgyas//
42a4(rgyud): sa gsum ye shes 'od po che//bde bar gshegs la phyag 'tshal lo//yon tan kun gyi 'byung gnas mchog/rig sngags bden ldan byin rlabs can//g.yung rung rtag pa dam chos

42a7(rgyud): sems can rnams la phan don phyir//phyag rgya 'phrin las ldan par gsungs//las kyi dri ma dang ldan zhing //nyon mongs nga rgyal gyis bcings nas//le lo ser sna'i 'dam rdzab can//
42b1(rgyud): khur dang ldan pa'i sems can rnams//myur du grol bar bya ba dang //thugs rje'i nyi ma bkram byas te//srid 'dam sdug po bskams pa dang //myur du sangs rgyas bsgrub pa'i phyir//
42b2(rgyud): lha dang gsang sngags bla ma dang //phrin las sbyor ba ldan pa dang //brgyud pa rgyun chad med pa dang //sbyor ba'i tshul 'byung bshad du gsol//rig sngags rjes gnang med pa ni//
42b3(rgyud): bsgrubs kyang 'bras bu med pa ste//'dren pa med pa'i shing rtas ni//'khor lo bzang yang gang du phyin//nyon mongs tsa ba bcod pa'i phyir//rig sngags ral gri rno dangs mtshungs//
42b4(rgyud): bdag 'dzin go cha 'phibs pa'i mdung //sdung bsngal mtsho skyems nyi ma che//kun rtog rdul phung bsreg pa'i me//shes rab lo thog 'khrungs pa'i rlan//nad dan kun sel sman mchog 
42b5(rgyud): ste//bgegs tshogs sel ba'i sred med bu//bag chags bkru ba'i chu rgyun te//longs spyod 'phel ba'i gter chen nyid//'khor ba las rgal gru gzings nyid//nyes pa kun sel

42b7(rgyud): 'gro la thugs rje chen po yis//sngags 'byung brgyud pa'i rim 'byung ba'i//chos kyi dam pa nyan brtson la//shin tu legs so lha'i bu//nyams dga' bde ba'i sa phyogs su//'gro 
43a1(rgyud): @#/ O /sa'i mnyam pa dkyil 'khor ltar//pad ma'i gdan 'dri'i dkyil 'khor ni//tshon sna ma lus rnams kyis bris//mgon po spyan ras gzigs dbang 
43a2(rgyud): gyi//sangs rgyas byang chub sems sogs dang //rig pa'i lha mo khro bor bcas//'jig rten skyong sogs ma lus pa'i//sku 'am phyag rgya bkod pa dang //mchod pa'i tshogs kyang bkram 
43a3(rgyud): par bya//mar me yon chag byug spos dri//zhal zas gtor ma sna tshogs dang //gzhan yang zas skom sna tshogs dang //rgyan dang na bza' lhab lhub dang //sil snyan me tog   
43a4(rgyud): phreng ba dang //yid 'od mdzes pa'i tshogs kyis brgyan//slob ma gus pas bla ma la//yon phul brtson byas 'di skad gsol//sems can kun gyi gnyen sems kyi//dmus 
43a5(rgyud): long lam log las 'dren pa//rab rib sel ba'i thur ma sogs//bdag lam 'dren pa'i sgron me sogs//shes rab snying rje'i skya ba can//thos bsam bsgom pa'i gru gzings

54a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa gsar pa btsir//nyal khar blugs na mig nad sos//mtshan mo skrangs la spru dkar ro//de kun nyi shu rtsa cig bzlas//brgya rtsa brgyad brjod shu dag dkar//

56b7(rgyud): na mo rat+na tra ya yA//na ma:Ar+ya a wa lo ke te shwa ra ya/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA satwAya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya//tad+ya thA/oM sarba d+harma Ni ma Ni tra ya//a ha ni pa ri shud d+ha satwA ya swAhA//
57a1(rgyud): @#/ O /yang na bsgom la 'jug pas ni//thog mar phyag rgya 'di bcing ngo //pus gtsugs spyi bor thal mo sbyar//'dzub gnyis bkug ste rtse sprad nas//de la 'theb 
57a2(rgyud): gnyis sbyar bar bya//'dis ni bdud las rgyal bar nges//yang na lag gnyis rgyab sprad nas//sor mo'i rtse rnams reg par bya//'theb gnyis rgyab nas 'dzub nang sbyar//smin  
57a3(rgyud): 'tshams su ni bzhag par bya//na mo rat+na tra ya yA//na mo:Ar+ya a wa lo ki te shwa ra ya/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA satwA ya//ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA//tad+ya thA/oM b+hu sarba tsakru ka ra ya//
57a4(rgyud): d+ha ra Ni ni Da ye swAhA/des ni bdud las rgyal 'gyur te//khyad par du ni lha mthong 'gyur//yang ni bsrangs te mnyam bzhag rgya//snying khar bzhag nas sngags brjod do//na mo rot+na tra 
57a5(rgyud): ya yA/na ma:ar+ya a wa lo ki te shwa raya//bo d+hi satwA ya/ma ha satwA yA/ma hA ka ru Ni ka yA//tad+ya thA/oM sarba duSh+Ta na mo bA ya bA swAhA//blo bur brtsod pa rgyal byed yin//yang 
57a6(rgyud): ni 'greng ste rting pa sprad//lag gnyis kyi ni mi 'jigs sbyin//rtsa sngags gang rung brjod pas ni//ting 'dzin yan lag brtan par 'gyur//yang na pad ma brgyan lung gi/rtsa ba'i   
57a7(rgyud): sngags kyi phyag rgya'o//lag gnyis thal sbyar srin lag gis//'the bo'i sen rtse mnan byas te//'the chu gi ni rtse mo sprad//gshegs su gsol ba'i phyag rgya ste//dgug na 'dzum 
57b1(rgyud): mo'i rtse mo dgug/yang na g.yas pa'i 'dzub mo dgug/g.yon pa srar btang bya ba'o//na mo rat+na tra ya yA//na maHAr+ya a wa lo ki te shwa ra ya/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA satwA yA//ma hA 
57b2(rgyud): ka ru Ni ka ya//tad+ya thA/oM sarba de ba a na ye swAhA//yang na sor mo rnams bkyed nas//'dzub mo g.yab pa lha rnams ni//spyan 'dren pa'i phyag rgya'o//na mo rat+na tra ya ya//na maHAr+ya
57b3(rgyud): a wa lo ki te shwa ra ya/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA satwA ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/tad+ya thA/oM pra smra e h+ya hi swAhA/ba ya ban phyag rgya 'di gsal la nyi zla gzas bzung dus su ni//gzungs sngag+sa
57b4(rgyud): yang dag bzlas pa yis//phyag rgya btab nas zos pas ni//shes rab 'phel dang tshe 'phel zhing //mi rnams gzugs bzang thob par nges//'greng ste rkang bshibs thal sbyar nas//
57b5(rgyud): 'theb gnyis lag pa'i nang bkug pa//snying khar bzhag bya spyan ras gzigs//yid bzhin nor bu'i 'khor lo'i rgya//mchod sogs las kyi sngon 'gro'o//de bzhin sdig
57b6(rgyud): bshags la yang ngo //'dzub bkug mthe bo sen mor bcas//lag gnyis rgyab sprad gar dbang rgya//thal sbyar 'theb 'dzub so sor brkyang //mgrin pa sngon po'i phyag rgya'o//
57b7(rgyud): 'dzub mo gnyis bkug lhag ma brkyang //mi bshol ba yi phyag rgya'o//lag gnyis rgyab sprad 'theb gnyis snol//bdug pa bzang la sngags 'di bzla//na mo rat+na tra ya yA/
58a1(rgyud): na maHAr+ya a ba lo ki te shwa ra yA/bo d+hi satwA ya/ma hA sa twA ya/ma hA ka ru Ni ka ya/tad+ya thA/a rad ne rad ne/ta ha sha rad na/ta ta sha rad na/pra ti ma he/rad 
58a2(rgyud): na da ka/de sa ha ha/snga bar brgya rtsa brjod do//nor yang thob cing gter mthong 'gyur//dgra gzhan bdag la byams 'dod na//so shing la ni nyer cig brjod//ldobs skyen 'dod pas shu dag la//
58a3(rgyud): stong ngam brgya bzlas snying khar bsku//gang bshad phyag rgya dang bcas pas//bdug pa'i sngags kyis bgegs kyang 'bros//na mo sarba bud d+ha d+harma saM g+ha ya/na maHar+ya a wa le ki te 
58a4(rgyud): shwa ra yA/bo d+hi sa twA ya/ma hA satwA ya/ma hA ka ru ི ka ya/tad+ya thA/oM hU bi hU bi/ka ya du bi pra sha ma ni swA hA//rnam grangs bdug pas sangs rgyas bdug/lha rnams dag kyang mchod 
58a5(rgyud): par bya//yang na lag g.yas mthe bo ni//lag 'thil bzhag nas sor mo dag/rna ltag phyir gsang 'dzub mo ni//brgya dang brkum bskul pa spros pa dang //g.yon pa mi 'jigs phyag rgya ste//
58a6(rgyud): dus ma yin par 'chi sogs bzlog/lag gnyis thal mo sbyar nas kyang //sor mo snol te bkan pa las//mthe bo mthe chung so sor ni//sprad de sbyar ba yon chab rgya//oM sarba saM sho 
58a7(rgyud): d+ha ne swA hA/de bzhin snol te bkan pa yi//gung gnyis nang du gshibs sgreng pa//mar me'i rgya ste sngags 'di'o//oM bud d+ha lo tsa na swA hA//de nyid sbubs nas gong mo gnyis//rtse sprad 
58b1(rgyud): sgrang bdug spos so//oM d+hu pa tra swAhA/ting sprad sor rtse rang sar 'dug/byug pa'i tshul ni byug pa'i rgya//oM d+ha li par+ya swAhA//gting sprad sor mo gyen du sprad//zhal nas 'thor tshul ba shos
58b2(rgyud): kyi rgya//oM ne pe t+ya pa Sha taM swAhA/las gnyis bkug nas rtse brgyad sprad//mthe bong gyen du bstan pa ni//na bza' dam pa'i phyag rgya'o//oM sarba b+ha sa ra ni swAhA/snyam pa kha phye bteg 
58b3(rgyud): pa'i tshul//gtor ma bstsal ba'i phyag rgya'o//oM oM kru hriHsa ga la g+ha na pa ri wa re naM/ba ri ke ra A a/a ta ran ti/a ba ta ran tu/dwa sha di ka lo ka lA la sa pa ri wa re b+h+ya/i daM 
58b4(rgyud): b+ha liM gri han tu/kha dan tu/bi dan tu/ya the baM/ya the ShAM/dza hUM baM to/san te sarba sa to ni/shan tiM kun ru/pu+iSh+TaM ku ru/rak+Sha taM ku ru/a ba ra na ku ru/gub ting ka rod ku rban tu hUM hUM phaT/

58b7(rgyud): ldan pa'i rnal 'byor grogs//lha dang 'jig rten skyong ba dang //'byung po yi dgas kun brtson 'gyur//sems kyi lcags kyu ldan byas te//nyon mongs ra ba nas drangs pa//lha  
59a1(rgyud): @#/ O /klu 'bum phrag sems dge ba'i//kun sleng byang chub rin chen gyi lam la bsgyur ba'i lhag bsam bskyed//mdzad pa rgya chen kun 'grub 'gyur//'phags pa spyan  
59a2(rgyud): ras gzigs dbang phyug gi rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po pad ma dra ba zhes bya ba las/sngags sbyor ba'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o////de nas 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor nas//byang chub 
59a3(rgyud): sems dpa' sems dpa' te//nyi ma'i snying po zhes bya ba//'khor gyi tshogs dang bcas pa zhig/mchod pa'i bkod pa chen po dang //bstod pa'i dbyangs dang bcas pa yi//bcom ldan

59a5(rgyud): ye shes rmad kyi 'byung gnas gtso//thar pa'i lam chen nyi shur rgyal//'dren pa'i (rgya)mtsho phyag 'tshal lo//mtshan dpe' mthar thugs dpag med sku//gzi brjid rgya che mkha' khyab pa//
59a6(rgyud): mkha' ltar yangs pa'i blo gros can//sku yi gsang ba phyag 'tshal lo//tshangs dbyangs du ma bsam mi khyab//namakha' yangs pa'i mtha' bkang nas//tshad med yan lag bcu gnyis 'byung //
59a7(rgyud): gsung gi gsang ba phyag 'tshal le//ting 'dzin rnam par bsam gtan sgo//bsam yas bsam du med bsams nas//bsam mi khyab pa'i shes rab dang //thugs rje'i thugs la 
59b1(rgyud): phyag 'tshal lo//'gro ba'i nyon mongs 'ching gcod cing //ma rig g.yul las rgyal byed pa//kun rtog dri ma sel ba yi//sgron me de ring bshad du gsol//byis rnams mu pa'i 'obs

60a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa lto yangs mgrin pa phra//kha yel zhabs tshad sha zhum pa la//shin tu gtsang mar bkrus nas kyang //ga pur gur gum cu gang dang //gla rtsi g+hi haM chu yis bkang //

61a7(rgyud): mi mdza' ba dang 'khrug pa dang //byad sdems drag po bzlog pa'am//sku la spos dang me tog mchod//bi na ya ka'i gzugs mdun du//dgra bo yi ni gzugs mdun du//sngags re mdud 
61b1(rgyud): pa brgya rtsa brgyad//mdud ldan g.yon gyi khro zhal btags//zhag bzhi lon nas skud pa bkrol//mdud pa re re'i bar bar du//sngags re brjod cing bcad par bya//bdag la sdang byed

62a1(rgyud): @#/ O /'bras bu gsum po zhib du btags//sbar dkar khye'u 'tsho'i nu zhor sbyar//bu tshes gsar pa'i bud med dang //khyi phag dag gis ma mthong bar//thum po 'dzub pa'i

63a1(rgyud): @#//thob||gsum gyis 'byung po dbang du 'gyur//bzhi pas gnod sbyin lnga pas klu//lnga pas lha min srin po rnams//drug pas mkha' lding ri bdun lha//bdun pas
